HBO Max: the complete help guide

Every help article HBO Max publishes for viewers, arranged for reading straight through

2026-08-04

HBO Max: the complete help guide

This guide gathers the help HBO Max publishes for viewers: 82 articles across 14 categories, in HBO Max’s own words, gathered on 04 August 2026. It covers signing up and signing in, plans and pricing, billing through a third party, profiles and parental controls, downloads and offline viewing, supported devices, accessibility settings, and what to do when something will not play.

The volume is the service, never the programmes. Nothing here is about what to watch.

HBO Max publishes its help in about two hundred country editions. This is the United States one; wording, prices and available plans differ elsewhere.

Account & Sign In: Manage Account

Change your account info

Change your account info

Mistyped email or can’t access email?

If you mistyped your email address when you created your account or you no longer have access to your email, please contact us. You can change your HBO Max account info (email, password, and name) on your phone, tablet, or computer. ##### What would you like to do? 1. Sign in to HBO Max on your phone, tablet, or computer. Can’t sign in? 2. Choose your profile and then Account. 3. Choose the Edit icon (image: pencil) next to Email Address. 4. Enter a new email address and choose Save. We’ll send an email to your current email address.Can’t access current email? If you don’t have access to your current email address, please contact us for help. 5. Check your inbox for an email from HBO Max (email subject: Please Confirm Email Address Change). This email should arrive within a few minutes. Didn’t get the email? 6. Open this email and choose Confirm Change.

Your email address is updated (sign in on all devices with your updated email address). 1. Sign in to HBO Max on your phone, tablet, or computer. Can’t sign in? 2. Choose your profile and then Account. 3. Choose the Edit icon (image: pencil) next to Password. 4. Enter your current password. Can’t update password?If you don’t know your password, you can reset your password instead. 5. Enter a new password and choose Save.

Your password is updated and your account is signed out of all devices. Now, sign in to HBO Max with your new password. You can add your mobile number as an additional sign-in method. Here’s how: 1. Sign in to HBO Max on your phone, tablet, or computer. Can’t sign in? 2. Choose your profile and then Account > Add Mobile Number. 3. We’ll send an email to your account email address. 4. Check your inbox for an email from HBO Max (email subject: Your One-Time HBO Max Code). This email should arrive within a few minutes. Didn’t get the email? 5. Enter the 6-digit code from the email. 6. Enter your mobile number and choose Verify Mobile Number. 7. Enter the 6-digit code from the text message that was sent to your mobile number.

Now you can sign in with your mobile number or email and password. If you added your mobile number to your account, here’s how to change it: 1. Sign in to HBO Max on your phone, tablet, or computer. Can’t sign in? 2. Choose your profile and then Account. 3. Choose the Edit icon (image: pencil) next to Mobile Number. 4. Enter your mobile number and choose Verify Mobile Number. You’ll get a text message with a verification code. 5. Enter the code from the text message and choose Save.

Now, sign in to HBO Max with your new mobile number. 1. Sign in to HBO Max on your phone, tablet, or computer. Can’t sign in? 2. Choose your profile and then Account. 3. Choose the Edit icon (image: pencil) next to Name. 4. Enter a new first and last name and choose Save.

Troubleshooting

If you get this message, it may mean that the email address that you entered is already associated with an HBO Max account. Double-check the email address that you entered and/or use a different email address. If you get this message, it may mean that the mobile number you entered is already associated with an HBO Max account. Make sure the mobile number you entered is correct. If you get this message, it means the password you entered doesn’t match what we have for your account. To resolve this, try the following: - Retype your current password (choose the Eye icon to reveal your password as you type it). - Reset your password

If you didn’t get a confirmation email from HBOMax@service.hbomax.com, try these things: - Search all inbox folders (including Promotions, Junk, and Spam) for an email from HBO Max (email subject: Please Confirm Email Address Change). - Try the Change email steps again with a different email address. Your new account email can’t be associated with another HBO Max account.

If you didn’t get a text message with a verification code, try the following: - Go back and repeat the Add or change mobile number steps above. - Choose Get New Code to send another text message. You should get a new text message within a couple of minutes. - Make sure your phone can receive text messages (check your cell phone signal strength and make sure Airplane mode is off). - Make sure your phone is not set to block messages from unknown senders.

If you still need help, please contact us.

HBO Max account sharing

HBO Max account sharing

HBO Max accounts are intended for the account owner (the person who created the account) and the people they live with. If you don’t live with the account owner, you need to sign up for your own HBO Max account. For more info, go to How do I subscribe to HBO Max? ##### Extra Member Add-On WarnerMedia-billed subscribers can share their HBO Max plan with someone who doesn’t live with them. For more info, go to Extra Member Add-On. ##### Account sharing Q&A You can share your HBO Max account with the people who live with you (the people in your household). People who don’t live with you (including family members living at another location, such as a college dorm), need to sign up for their own HBO Max account. WarnerMedia-billed subscribers can share their HBO Max plan with someone who doesn’t live with them. For more info, go to Extra Member Add-On. If you share your account credentials with people who don’t live with you, it will affect your ability to access HBO Max in the future. No, HBO Max accounts are intended for the account owner (the person who created the account) and the people they live with. People who don’t live with you (including family members living at another location, such as a college dorm), need to sign up for their own HBO Max account. Extra Member Add-On If your subscription is billed through us (WarnerMedia), you can share your HBO Max plan with someone who doesn’t live with you. For more info, go to Extra Member Add-On. Student subscriptions Some colleges and universities include access to HBO Max. To find out if a college or university offers HBO Max, go to HBO Max providers. A discounted HBO Max plan is available to eligible students. For more info, go to HBO Max student discount. HBO Max accounts are intended for the account owner (the person who created the account) and the people they live with. ##### Don’t live with the account owner? If you don’t live with the account owner, choose Subscribe Now or Get Started to sign up for your own HBO Max account. If you’re the account owner (or live with the account owner), do the following: 1. Choose Home if the device you’re using is connected to your home network and Traveling if you’re away from home.Note The number of times you can choose Home or Traveling is limited. 2. Choose Send Code and we’ll send a code to the account email.(If your email address isn’t associated with the HBO Max account, you’ll need to complete these steps with the account owner.) 3. Check your inbox for an email from HBO Max with the subject: Your One-Time HBO Max Code. This email should arrive within a few minutes. Be sure to search all inbox folders (including Promotions, Junk, and Spam). 4. Open the most recent One-Time Code email and enter the code from the email.If the code doesn’t work, choose Get New Code to get another code emailed to you.

Now you can continue streaming HBO Max. ##### Mistyped email or can’t access email? If you mistyped your email address when you created your account or you no longer have access to your email, please reach out to us by selecting Contact Us in the lower-right corner of this page. To make sure only you and the people you live with have access to your account, review the list of devices using your account. Here’s how: 1. Sign in to HBO Max on your phone, tablet, or computer. Can’t sign in? 2. Choose your profile, then Account > Devices. 3. Review the list of devices signed in to your account, including the profile name, sign-in date, and country.If you don’t recognize a device or no longer own a device, choose X beside the device to sign your account out of the device.

You can also change your account password at HBOMax.com/account/edit/password. This will sign your account out of all devices. For more info, go to Change your account info. Your HBO Max household is a collection of devices that can stream HBO Max using the same account. To identify which devices belong to your household, we use a mix of account details, profile settings, network data, and device information—such as IP addresses, device IDs, and user activity—based on our discretion. You can update your HBO Max household on a TV device connected to your home network. To find out how, see the next section. You can update your HBO Max household on a TV device connected to your home network. Once set, devices connected to the same home network as your TV will be included in your HBO Max household. Here’s how to update your HBO Max household: 1. Open the HBO Max app on a TV connected to your home network.Don’t have a TV? Go to HBOMax.com/account on a computer connected to your home network, then skip ahead to Step 3. 2. Choose the Settings icon (image: cog (lower left)) (lower left) and then Account. 3. Choose Update HBO Max Household. 4. Choose Send Code and we’ll send a code to your account email address. 5. Check your inbox for an email from HBO Max with the subject: Your One-Time HBO Max Code. This email should arrive within a few minutes. Be sure to search all inbox folders (including Promotions, Junk, and Spam). 6. Open the most recent One-Time Code email and enter the code in the app.If the code doesn’t work, choose Get New Code to get another code emailed to you.Sharing the codeDo not share the code with people outside your household as it may affect your ability to access HBO Max in the future.

Devices connected to the same home network as the device used in Step 1, will be included in your HBO Max Household. ##### Travel often or live in more than one location? You can only update your household a limited number of times. If you travel often or split your time between multiple residences, go to Troubleshooting. ##### Need to change your email? To change your account email, go to Change account info. If you no longer have access to your email, please reach out to us by selecting Contact Us in the lower-right corner of this page. ##### Troubleshooting If you’re the account owner and you’re repeatedly prompted for your location (Home or Traveling), do the following: - Update your HBO Max household on a TV device connected to your home network. To find out how, go to Update your HBO Max household.

If updating your household doesn’t help, go to Not sure if someone has access to your account? If you live in more than one location, try this: 1. Connect a mobile device (like your phone) to your home network—the same network your TV uses. 2. Open the HBO Max app and stream a few minutes of content at each residence.

This helps HBO Max recognize both locations as part of your household. Repeat every 90 days. If you’re traveling within the U.S. and get the message ‘Subscribe today to keep streaming,’ select I’m Traveling to continue watching HBO Max. Please note that the number of times you can choose ‘I’m Traveling’ is limited. To avoid future interruptions, do the following: 1. When you’re at home, open the HBO Max app on your phone or tablet (or go to HBOMax.com on your computer). 2. Make sure your device is connected to your home Wi-Fi network. 3. Stream a few minutes of content at least once a month.

This helps HBO Max recognize your device as part of your household. - On the TV device where you’re streaming, choose I’m traveling. - If that option is no longer available, reach out to us by selecting Contact Us in the lower-right corner of this page.

When you select Send Code on the Home or Traveling screen, we’ll email a one-time code to the email associated with the account (subject line: Your One-Time HBO Max Code). The email should arrive within a few minutes. Make sure to check all folders in your inbox, including Promotions, Junk, and Spam. If you’re the account owner and didn’t get the email, please reach out to us by selecting Contact Us in the lower-right corner of this page. The number of times you can choose ‘I’m Traveling’ is limited. If you haven’t marked your account as traveling several times, someone outside your household may have marked your account as traveling. Not sure if someone has access to your account? If you think you’re getting this message by mistake, reach out to us by selecting Contact Us in the lower-right corner of this page. This message appears when your HBO Max household has been updated several times within a short period. If you haven’t updated your HBO Max household several times, someone outside your household may have updated your household. Not sure if someone has access to your account? If you think you’re getting this message by mistake, please reach out to us by selecting Contact Us in the lower-right corner of this page.

HBO Max Privacy and Ad Choices

HBO Max Privacy and Ad Choices

Your privacy is important to us. We’ve gathered some info that should help. The HBO Max Privacy Policy outlines what information we collect, how we use it, and where and how we share it. Our Privacy Policy is available at: HBOMax.com/privacy. Our Children’s Privacy Policy is available at: HBOMax.com/privacy/children. There are a number of tools and options you can use to help control how your information is collected, used, and disclosed. To learn more, go to How can I control my information? We personalize some ad experiences on HBO Max. For more information and to make choices about targeted advertising, go to Advertising Choices. To learn about ads during shows and movies, go to Ads on HBO Max. ##### Allow the sale or sharing of personal information Some US residents can stop the sale or sharing of personal information with third parties. For more info, view the HBO Max Privacy Policy. Some US residents can also submit a Do Not Sell or Share privacy request on the Warner Bros. Discovery Privacy Center. Submitting a Do Not Sell or Share privacy request affects data collected from all Warner Bros. Discovery products and services (not just HBO Max). For more info, go to wbdprivacy.com/do-not-sell-or-share-request. We periodically email you show and movie recommendations, product updates, and special offers. If you don’t want to receive these emails, you can turn off Emails from HBO Max and Affiliates in Settings. For more info, go to HBO Max settings. You’ll still receive service emails to your account (such as when you make changes to your password or sign in on a new device). App Notifications: You might see pop-up notifications on your phone or tablet about new HBO Max shows and movies, as well as other suggestions or notices. You can turn off App Notifications in App Settings on your phone or tablet. In-app notifications: You’ll find personalized suggestions in the HBO Max mobile app. To review the suggestions, choose the Bell icon (image: bell) in the upper-right corner of HBO Max (phones and tablets only). In-app notifications are only available with Adult profiles. ##### Things to know - Deleting your account will not delete the app from any devices. - If you create a new HBO Max account with the same email address, it won’t have your previous settings, downloads, and watch history.

Here’s how to delete your HBO Max account: ###### Step 1: Cancel your subscription You’ll need to cancel your subscription before you submit a delete request. To find out how to cancel, go to How do I cancel HBO Max? You can continue streaming HBO Max until your subscription expires. ###### Step 2: Submit a delete request Once your HBO Max subscription has expired, you can submit a delete request. Choose a device: 1. On your phone or tablet, open the HBO Max app. 2. Sign in (if needed) and choose your profile. Can’t sign in? 3. Choose Privacy & Legal and then Delete My Account. The app will open a browser window. 4. Enter the information associated with your account and submit the form. If you don’t know your HBO Max account email, go to Forgot my HBO Max email. 5. Check your inbox for an email on behalf of HBO Max (the subject line is: Your request to delete your account). 6. Open this email and choose Delete My Account.

  1. Open a browser and go to HBOMax.com/account-delete.
  2. Enter the information associated with your account and submit the form. If you don’t know your HBO Max account email, go to Forgot my HBO Max email.
  3. Check your inbox for an email on behalf of HBO Max (the subject line is: Your request to delete your account).
  4. Open this email and choose Delete My Account.

Once you complete these steps, we’ll delete your HBO Max account if the account qualifies. The account deletion process usually takes 4 to 5 weeks. You’ll receive an email letting you know when your account has been deleted. ##### Account topics - Help keep your account secure - Change your email or password - Stop unwanted use of your account

Help keep your account secure

Help keep your account secure

Here are some recommendations to help keep your HBO Max account secure. ##### Personal information HBO Max will never ask for personal information such as your HBO Max account password or payment information. If you receive any communication (e.g. texts, emails, social posts, or phone calls) that ask for personal information, it’s likely a phishing scam. Here’s how to spot phishing attempts: - You’re asked to purchase something (including gift cards, pre-paid credit cards, or crypto currency). - You’re asked to participate in an offer that’s exclusive only to you. - You’re asked to pay through a third-party website. - You’re asked to click on a URL you haven’t visited before. If you clicked on a link, don’t enter any information.

If you’re concerned you’ve accidentally shared personal information with a party pretending to be HBO Max, here’s what you can do: - Contact your local authorities and bank to report any fraudulent activity. - Contact us to share the details of the phishing attempt.

More security tips

You can help protect your account by following these suggestions: ###### Password recommendations Your password should be: - Unique to HBO Max. Don’t use a password that you use with other websites or apps. - At least 10 characters with no more than 4 repeating characters in a row.

To help keep your HBO Max account secure, it’s a good idea to periodically change your password. Need help changing your account password? Go to Change your email or password. ###### Don’t share your account info Members of your household can sign in to your account and stream HBO Max on different devices. Your account email and password should not be shared with anyone outside your household. If you think your account is being used without your permission, go to Stop unwanted use of your account. ###### Sign out of unused devices Regularly review the list of devices using your account and remove devices that you no longer use or own. For more info, go to Manage your devices. ###### Check your account email address Make sure your account email address is correct. This way you’ll get important emails about your account—like the email we send if you need to reset your password. Need help changing your account email? Go to Change your email or password.

Manage your HBO Max devices

Manage your HBO Max devices

When someone in your household signs in on a device with your account, HBO Max adds the device to your account. The number of devices you can stream on at the same time depends on your subscription plan. ##### Manage devices using your account To review the devices that are currently signed in to your account: 1. Sign in to HBO Max on your phone, tablet, or computer. Can’t sign in? 2. Choose your profile, then Account > Devices.(Only Adult profiles can manage devices.) 3. Review the list of devices signed in to your account, including the profile name, sign-in date, and country.If you don’t recognize a device, go to Stop unwanted use of your account.

Devices are automatically added to the device list when you sign in (and are removed when you sign out). We recommend signing out of devices that your household no longer uses. When someone in your household signs in with your account, HBO Max adds the device to your account. To add a device to your account, install the HBO Max app and sign in. If you no longer own or use a device, you can remove the device from your account by signing out. You need to sign out of devices used by people who don’t live with you. Here’s how: 1. Sign in to HBO Max on your phone, tablet, or computer. Can’t sign in? 2. Choose your profile, then Account > Devices.(Only Adult profiles can manage devices.) 3. Choose the X beside the device you want to sign out of.

Note If someone in your household is streaming, they won’t be signed out of HBO Max until the end of their show or movie. If you think someone might have access to your HBO Max account without your permission, we recommend signing out of all devices. Here’s how: 1. Sign in to HBO Max on your phone, tablet, or computer. Can’t sign in? 2. Choose your profile, then Account > Devices.(Only Adult profiles can manage devices.) 3. Choose Sign Out All Devices.

Note If someone in your household is streaming, they won’t be signed out of HBO Max until the end of their show or movie. In HBO Max, do one of the following: - Phone, tablet, or computer: Choose your profile (upper right) and then Sign Out. - TV device: Choose the Settings icon (image: cog) and then Sign Out.

Stop unwanted use of your account

Stop unwanted use of your account

If you think someone might have access to your HBO Max account without your permission, we recommend changing your password. This will sign your account out of all devices. For a list of devices that have access to your account, go to Manage your devices. ##### Change your password To change your HBO Max account password: 1. Sign in to HBO Max on your phone, tablet, or computer. Can’t sign in? 2. Choose your profile and then Account. 3. Choose the Edit icon (image: pencil) next to Password. 4. Enter your current password.If you don’t know your password, you can reset your password instead. 5. Enter a new password and choose Save.

Your password is updated and your account is signed out of all devices. Now, sign in to HBO Max with your new password. ##### ‘We couldn’t update your password’ message If you get this message, it means the password you entered doesn’t match what we have for your account. To resolve this, try the following: - Retype your current password (choose the Eye icon to reveal your password as you type it). - Reset your password

For account safety tips, go to Help keep your account secure.

Account & Sign In: Profiles

Add, change, or reset a Profile PIN

Add, change, or reset a Profile PIN

You can lock your Adult profile so that a PIN is required to access your profile. An Adult profile is any profile with Kids Mode turned off. When your profile is locked, your 4-digit Profile PIN must be entered to access your profile. Locked profiles have a Lock icon (image: closed padlock) beside the avatar. ##### Choose a topic: You can keep other people in your household from using your Adult profile by locking your profile. To lock or unlock your profile, choose the device you’re using: 1. In HBO Max, choose your profile and then Manage Profiles. 2. Choose an Adult profile (a profile with Kids Mode turned off). 3. To lock or unlock your profile, choose Lock Profile. 4. If prompted, enter your account password and choose Submit.To reset your password, go to Forgot my password. 5. If prompted, enter a 4-digit Profile PIN and choose Save. 6. Choose Done.

When your profile is locked, you’ll need to enter your Profile PIN to switch to your profile. 1. In HBO Max, choose your profile. 2. Do one of the following:Choose the Edit icon (image: pencil) below the Adult profile you want to edit. 3. Apple TV: Choose Edit and then the Adult profile you want to edit. 4. To lock or unlock your profile, choose Lock Profile. 5. If prompted, enter your account password. To open the on-screen keyboard, press the Select or OK button on your remote.To reset your password, go to Forgot my password. 6. If prompted, enter a 4-digit Profile PIN and choose Save. 7. Scroll down and choose Done.

When your profile is locked, you’ll need to enter your Profile PIN to switch to your profile. Choose the device you’re using: 1. In HBO Max, choose your profile and then Manage Profiles. 2. Choose an Adult profile (a profile with Kids Mode turned off). 3. Choose Reset Profile PIN (this option is only available if you previously locked your profile). 4. Enter your account password and choose Submit.To reset your password, go to Forgot my password. 5. Enter a new 4-digit PIN and choose Save. 6. Choose Done.

  1. In HBO Max, choose your profile.
  2. Do one of the following:Choose the Edit icon (image: pencil) below the Adult profile you want to edit.
  3. Apple TV: Choose Edit and then the Adult profile you want to edit.
  4. Choose Reset Profile PIN (this option is only available if you previously locked your profile).
  5. Enter your account password. To open the on-screen keyboard, press the Select or OK button on your remote.To reset your password, go to Forgot my password.
  6. Enter a new 4-digit PIN and choose Save.
  7. Scroll down and choose Done.

Choose the device you’re using: 1. When you’re prompted for your Profile PIN, choose Forgot PIN? The app will open a browser window. 2. Choose Reset Profile PIN. 3. Enter your account password and choose Next. 4. Tap the first box, enter a new PIN, and then choose Save. 5. Scroll down and choose Done. 6. Close the browser window and go back to the HBO Max app.

  1. When you’re prompted for your Profile PIN, choose Forgot PIN?
  2. Enter your account password.TV device: To open the on-screen keyboard, press the Select or OK button on your remote.To reset your password, go to Forgot my password.
  3. Enter a new PIN and choose Save.

When you add, change, or reset a Profile PIN, we’ll send an email to your account email address. ##### Parent Code You can turn on Kid-Proof Exit to keep your child from leaving their Kids profile. For more info, go to Kid-Proof Exit and Parent Code.

All about Kids profiles

All about Kids profiles

There are two types of profiles: Adult and Kids. Your account can have up to 5 profiles. You can create a Kids profile that limits what content ratings your child can watch. Note You may be prompted to add a birthdate to your Kids profile. ##### Why create a Kids profile? - Parental controls. You can choose what content ratings your child can watch. - Kids Home screen. There are different Home screen experiences for Kids profiles. Your child can also have their own My List and Continue Watching list. - To keep your child from leaving their profile, turn on Kid-Proof Exit (or add a Profile PIN to your Adult profiles). - Kids profiles can’t access account management features, like your password and subscription information.

Choose a topic:

Here’s how to add a Kids profile: 1. In HBO Max, choose your profile and then + New Profile (you may need to scroll if you have more than 3 profiles). 2. Select an avatar or choose Skip. 3. Enter your child’s name (between 1 and 14 characters). 4. Turn on Kids Mode. 5. Choose a Ratings group. By default, Ratings are set to PG / TV-PG. For more info, go to Content ratings. 6. Kid-Proof Exit (optional): Turn on this option to help keep your child from leaving their profile. Find out more at Kid-Proof Exit and Parent Code. 7. Add your child’s birthdate and choose Save.

Once saved, you can edit the profile to make changes. ##### Add a Profile PIN You can also add a Profile PIN to any Adult profile so that your children can’t watch HBO Max using that Adult profile. Choose the device you’re using: 1. In HBO Max, choose your profile and then Manage Profiles. 2. Choose a Kids profile. 3. Here you can change the profile’s settings (e.g ratings, or Kid-Proof Exit) or delete the profile. If you need to change the birthdate, please contact us. 4. Choose Done.

  1. At HBOMax.com, choose your profile and then Manage Profiles.

  2. Choose a Kids profile.

  3. Here you can change the profile’s avatar, name, ratings, Kids Mode, Kid-Proof Exit, and delete the profile. If you need to change the birthdate, please contact us.

  4. Choose Done.

  5. Choose the Edit icon (image: pencil) below a Kids profile.

  6. Here you can change the profile’s avatar, name, ratings, Kids Mode, Kid-Proof Exit, and delete the profile. If you need to change the birthdate, please contact us.

  7. Choose Done.

Apple TV: Choose Edit and then the Kids profile you want to edit. ##### Turning off Kids Mode You can’t turn off Kids Mode if the profile’s birthdate is under 18. Kid-Proof Exit: Turn on Kid-Proof Exit to keep your child from leaving their Kids profile. Find out more at Kid-Proof Exit and Parent Code. Here’s how to switch to another profile: 1. In HBO Max, choose your profile. 2. Choose a different profile. 3. If prompted, choose Exit Kids Profile and enter your 4-digit Parent Code to exit the Kids profile.

If you switch to an Adult profile with a Profile PIN, you’ll need to enter the 4-digit PIN. ###### ‘Who’s Watching’ screen Here’s what happens when you open HBO Max: - TV device: If the last profile used was a Kids profile, HBO Max will open with this profile. If the last profile used was an Adult profile, you’ll see the ‘Who’s Watching’ screen. - Computer, phone, or tablet: HBO Max always opens with the last profile used (you won’t see the ‘Who’s Watching’ screen).

You can add a Profile PIN to any Adult profile so that your children can’t watch HBO Max using that Adult profile. When you edit a Kids profile, you can choose a content rating to limit what your child can watch and find with search. For more info about ratings, go to Content ratings on HBO Max. When a Kids profile is selected, the Home screen and search results only include shows and movies with ratings that the Kids profile is allowed to watch. The default ratings for a new Kids profile are PG / TV-PG. To change ratings for a Kids profile, edit the Kids profile. There are different Home screen experiences for Kids profiles based on the content ratings. On the Kids Home screen, Kids profile users can discover titles by selecting their favorite character’s picture. The Kids Home screen also includes Continue Watching and My List. ##### Default Kids profile New HBO Max accounts have a default Kids profile that can watch TV-PG and PG titles. You can edit or delete this profile.

Create or change HBO Max profiles

Create or change HBO Max profiles

For a more personalized experience, you can create up to 5 profiles for everyone in your household. Profiles keep track of what you’ve watched and what you’d like to watch. There are two types of profiles: Adult and Kids. For info about Kids profiles, go to All about Kids profiles. Each profile has its own: - Avatar - My List and Continue Watching list - Settings - App language - Ratings - Recommendations based on your watch history - Parental controls (optional) - PIN (optional)

Choose a topic:
  1. In HBO Max, choose your profile and then + New Profile (you may need to scroll right if you have more than 3 profiles).
  2. Select an avatar or choose Skip.
  3. Enter a name (between 1 and 14 characters).
  4. To create a Kids profile, turn on Kids Mode, choose a Ratings group, and add your child’s birthdate.
  5. If prompted, add a birthdate and gender (Adult profiles only).
  6. Choose Save.

Once saved, you can edit your profile to make changes. Note Extra member accounts are limited to 1 profile. For more info, go to Extra Member Add-On. ##### Personalize your profile The first time you use a new Adult profile, you can personalize your experience by choosing your favorite genres, brands, shows, and movies. To get started, choose Let’s Go. If you don’t want to share your preferences, choose Skip to start streaming. Choose the device you’re using: 1. Choose your profile and then Manage Profiles. 2. Choose the profile you want to change. 3. Make changes to the profile and choose Done.

Note You can’t turn off Kids Mode if the profile’s birthdate is under 18. An Adult profile can’t be changed to a Kids profile. 1. At HBOMax.com, choose your profile and then Manage Profiles. 2. Choose the profile you want to change. 3. Make changes to the profile and choose Done.

Note You can’t turn off Kids Mode if the profile’s birthdate is under 18. An Adult profile can’t be changed to a Kids profile. 1. In HBO Max, choose your profile. 2. Choose the Edit icon (image: pencil) below the profile you want to change.Note You can’t change the birthdate for a profile 3. Make changes to the profile and choose Done.

Apple TV: Choose Edit and then the profile you want to edit. Note You can’t turn off Kids Mode if the profile’s birthdate is under 18. An Adult profile can’t be changed to a Kids profile. You can change the ratings for an Adult profile at HBOMax.com/editprofiles. Choose the device you’re using: 1. In HBO Max, choose your profile and then Manage Profiles. 2. Choose a profile. 3. Choose Delete Profile and confirm the deletion.This will delete the profile and its info, such as My List and Continue Watching. 4. Choose Done.

Can’t delete a profile? You can’t delete the active profile. Switch to another profile before repeating the steps above. You also can’t delete your first profile (created with your account), but you can edit it. 1. In HBO Max, choose your profile. 2. Choose the Edit icon (image: pencil) below a profile. 3. Choose Delete Profile and confirm the deletion.This will delete the profile and its info, such as My List and Continue Watching. 4. Choose Done.

Apple TV: Choose Edit and then the profile you want to edit. Can’t delete a profile? You can’t delete the active profile. Switch to another profile before repeating the steps above. You also can’t delete your first profile (created with your account), but you can edit it. Here’s how to switch to another profile: 1. In HBO Max, choose your profile. 2. Choose a different profile. 3. If prompted, choose Exit Kids Profile and enter your 4-digit Parent Code to exit the Kids profile.

If you switch to an Adult profile with a Profile PIN, you’ll need to enter the 4-digit PIN. To find out how to transfer an Adult profile to another account, go to Transfer a profile. You can change the language of text in the HBO Max app. Choose the device you’re using: 1. Choose your profile and then Manage Profiles. 2. Choose a profile. 3. Choose Display Language, select a language, and then choose Done.

The display language is saved with your profile and used across all devices. 1. Choose your profile. 2. Choose the Edit icon (image: pencil) below a profile. 3. Choose Display Language, select a language, and then choose Done.

Apple TV: Choose Edit and then the profile you want to edit. The display language is saved with your profile and used across all devices. Here’s what happens when you open HBO Max: - TV device: If the last profile used was a Kids profile, HBO Max will open with this profile. If the last profile used was an Adult profile, you’ll see the ‘Who’s Watching’ screen. - Phone, tablet, or computer: HBO Max always opens with the last profile used (you won’t see the ‘Who’s Watching’ screen).

You can add a Profile PIN to any Adult profile so that your children can’t watch HBO Max using that Adult profile. If you need help resetting your Parent Code, go to Kid-Proof Exit and Parent Code.

HBO Max Kid-Proof Exit and Parent Code

HBO Max Kid-Proof Exit and Parent Code

Kid-Proof Exit is an optional setting to keep your child from leaving their Kids profile. When this option is turned on, a 4-digit Parent Code is required to exit the Kids profile and switch to another profile. Your account has just one Parent Code. A Profile PIN is an optional setting to keep other members of your household from using a profile. For more info, go to Add or change your Profile PIN. ##### Choose a topic: When Kid-Proof Exit is turned on, you’ll need a 4-digit Parent Code to exit the Kids profile. Here’s how to add or reset your account’s Parent Code. Choose the device you’re using: 1. Go to play.hbomax.com/parent-code and sign in (if needed).Need help signing in? 2. Enter a new 4-digit Parent Code and choose Save.

Your Parent Code applies to all Kids profiles with Kid-Proof Exit turned on. 1. In HBO Max, choose your profile. 2. Choose the Edit icon (image: pencil) below a Kids profile. 3. Turn on Kid-Proof Exit. 4. Create Parent Code (if applicable) and enter a 4-digit code, then Save. 5. Choose Reset Parent Code (if applicable) and enter a 4-digit code, then Save. 6. Choose Done.

Your Parent Code applies to all Kids profiles with Kid-Proof Exit turned on. Choose the device you’re using: 1. If your account doesn’t have a Parent Code, go to Add or reset a Parent Code. 2. Go to HBOMax.com/editprofiles and sign in (if needed).Need help signing in? 3. Choose a Kids profile. 4. Turn Kid-Proof Exit on or off, then choose Done.

When Kid-Proof Exit is turned on, a 4-digit Parent Code is required to exit the Kids profile. 1. In HBO Max, choose your profile. 2. Choose the Edit icon (image: pencil) below a Kids profile. 3. Turn Kid-Proof Exit on or off. 4. Choose Reset Parent Code (if applicable) and enter a 4-digit code, then Save. 5. Choose Done.

When Kid-Proof Exit is turned on, a 4-digit Parent Code is required to exit the Kids profile. ##### Troubleshooting Here’s how to reset your Parent Code: 1. Go to play.hbomax.com/parent-code and sign in (if needed).Need help signing in? 2. Enter a new 4-digit Parent Code and choose Save.

If you forgot your Parent Code, do the following: 1. When prompted to enter your Parent Code, choose Forgot Code? 2. Enter your account password.

If you forgot or don’t know your HBO Max password, try resetting your password. If this doesn’t help, go to Can’t reset password.

Transfer an HBO Max profile

Transfer an HBO Max profile

You can transfer an Adult profile to a new or existing HBO Max account. This includes: - Your avatar - Watch history - My List - Your PIN - Settings, recommendations, and more

For more info, go to HBO Max profiles. Note The account with the profile you want to transfer needs to have an active subscription. ##### Things to know - Kids profiles can’t be transferred to another account. - Both accounts must be from the same country or region. - Accounts can have up to 5 profiles(Extra Member accounts are limited to 1 profile).

Transfer a profile to a new or existing account

If transferring a profile to an existing account, you’ll need to sign in to the destination account. The transfer creates a copy of the profile in the destination account. To transfer a profile to another account: 1. On your computer, go to HBOMax.com and sign in to the account that has the profile you want to transfer. This account must have an active subscription. 2. Choose your profile and then Manage Profiles. 3. Choose the Adult profile you want to transfer. 4. Choose Transfer Profile. 5. Choose Get Started.(You’ll be signed out of your account.) 6. Choose your destination account:New account: Enter an email address for the new account. Complete the account form and choose Create Account. Next, you’ll choose a subscription plan, add your payment info, and start your subscription. 7. Existing account: Enter the email address of the destination account. Then, enter the account password and choose Sign In. 8. Confirm the profile transfer.

You’ll be signed in to the destination account once the transfer is complete. ##### Delete the original profile Transferring a profile doesn’t remove it from the original account. If you’d like, you can delete the profile afterward. ##### Troubleshooting If the destination account is an Extra Member account, you’ll be prompted to replace the existing profile (Extra Member accounts are limited to 1 profile). Choose Replace & Transfer to replace the existing profile on the Extra Member account. If the destination account doesn’t have an active HBO Max subscription, you’ll be prompted to subscribe before completing the transfer. Choose Subscribe Now to finish the transfer. The destination account already has the maximum number of profiles. To proceed with the transfer, you’ll need to delete one of the existing profiles. Here’s how: 1. Go to HBOMax.com and sign in to the destination account. 2. Choose your profile and then Manage Profiles. 3. Choose a profile and then Delete Profile.This will permanently remove the profile and its data (e.g. My List, Continue Watching). 4. Choose Done. 5. Restart the profile transfer process.

Can’t delete a profile?
  • You can’t delete the active profile. Switch to another one first.
  • The first profile created with the account can’t be deleted, but it can be edited.

Account & Sign In: Sign In

Connect your provider

Connect your provider

If you get HBO Max through an internet, mobile, or TV provider, you need to connect your provider account (with your HBO Max subscription) before signing in to HBO Max for the first time. ##### Things to know - If you already have an HBO Max account, sign in with your account email and password. - If you get HBO Max through the Disney+, Hulu, HBO Max Bundle or Philo, go to Activate HBO Max then sign in.

Choose the device you’re using:

Here’s how to connect your provider: 1. Open HBO Max and choose Sign in.Already signed in? Choose your profile and then Sign Out. 2. On the sign-in screen, choose create an HBO Max account (below Don’t have an HBO Max account yet?).iPhone or iPadChoose Allow to let HBO Max use your TV provider info from iOS Settings. Or, choose Don’t Allow to select your provider in HBO Max. 3. Choose who you get HBO Max (or HBO) through or search for your provider.My provider isn’t listed. 4. On your provider’s sign-in screen, enter your provider account info and sign in.Need help? Go to Troubleshooting. 5. Enter an email address for your HBO Max account.Already have an account? Enter the email address associated with your HBO Max account. 6. If prompted, do one of the following:New subscribers: Complete the account form and choose Create Account. 7. Returning subscribers: Check your inbox for an email sent by HBO Max (email subject: Time Sensitive: Your One-Time HBO Max Code) and enter the 6-digit code from your email. This email should arrive within a few minutes. Be sure to search all inbox folders (including Promotions, Junk, and Spam). 8. Choose who’s watching and you’re ready to stream.

If you added a password to your HBO Max account, you can sign in on any supported device using your HBO Max email and password. ###### Troubleshooting If you get this message, make sure HBO Max is included with your internet, mobile, or TV package. To do this, sign in to your internet, mobile, or TV provider account to check the status of your HBO Max subscription. If you need help, contact your provider. For more info, go to Fix HBO Max subscription issue. Apple iPhone or iPad If you get this message on your iPhone or iPad, try removing your TV provider from your iOS settings. Here’s how: 1. Close the HBO Max app. 2. On your Apple iPhone or iPad, go to Settings > General > TV Provider. Under Allow TV provider to access, turn off HBO Max. 3. Open HBO Max and try connecting your provider again.

If you get this message when connecting your provider, it means your provider account is already linked to an HBO Max account. Instead of connecting your provider, try signing in with your HBO Max account email and password. If you’re not sure which email is associated with your HBO Max account, go to Find your account email. If you can’t sign in to your internet, mobile, or TV provider account, make sure you can sign in on your provider’s website. Here’s how: 1. Go to your internet, mobile, or TV provider’s website. 2. Sign in with your provider account.Note If you can’t sign in, look for an option to reset your password. If this doesn’t help, please contact your provider for help. HBO Max Support doesn’t have access to your provider sign-in information. 3. After you successfully sign in on your provider’s website, go back to HBO Max and try the Connect your provider steps again.

Troubleshooting

If you get this message, make sure HBO Max is included with your internet, mobile, or TV package. To do this, sign in to your internet, mobile, or TV provider account to check the status of your HBO Max subscription. If you need help, contact your provider. For more info, go to Fix HBO Max subscription issue. Apple TV If you get this message on your Apple TV, try removing your TV provider from your tvOS settings: 1. Close the HBO Max app. 2. On your Apple TV, go to Settings > Users and Accounts > TV Provider. Under Allow TV provider to access, turn off HBO Max. 3. Open HBO Max and try connecting your provider again.

If the 6-digit code isn’t working, here are some things to try: - Open a private or incognito browser window and then go to HBOMax.com/providers. - Try using a different supported browser (Chrome, Firefox, Microsoft Edge, or Safari). - Try scanning the QR code on your TV with your phone’s camera app (instead of going to HBOMax.com/providers).

If this doesn’t help, try the Connect provider steps on a different device (computer or phone). Once you connect to your provider (and create an HBO Max account), you can sign in on your TV using your HBO Max email and password. If you get this message when connecting your provider, it means your provider account is already linked to an HBO Max account. Instead of connecting your provider, try signing in with your HBO Max account email and password. If you’re not sure which email is associated with your HBO Max account, go to Find your account email. If you can’t sign in to your internet, mobile, or TV provider account, make sure you can sign in on your provider’s website. Here’s how: 1. Go to your internet, mobile, or TV provider’s website. 2. Sign in with your provider account.Note If you can’t sign in, look for an option to reset your password. If this doesn’t help, please contact your provider for help. HBO Max Support doesn’t have access to your provider sign-in information. 3. After you successfully sign in on your provider’s website, go back to HBO Max and try the Connect your provider steps again.

If you have an active HBO Max subscription and get a Welcome back message, go to Fix an HBO Max subscription issue. ##### Your provider needs you to sign in again You may need to periodically sign in to your internet, mobile, or TV provider account that includes access to HBO Max. If you’re asked to sign in again, choose Connect Your Provider, then choose your provider, and sign in to your provider account. Still need help? Go to Can’t connect provider.

Sign in to HBO Max

Sign in to HBO Max

To sign in, choose the device you’re using below. ##### Choose the device you’re using: Here’s how to sign in to the HBO Max app: 1. Open HBO Max and choose Sign In.Already signed in? Choose your profile (upper right) and then Sign Out. 2. Enter your HBO Max account email or mobile number and choose Continue.Don’t have an HBO Max account yet? If you get HBO Max (or HBO) through an internet, mobile, or TV provider, choose create an HBO Max account and then choose your provider. Need help? Go to Connect your provider. 3. Do one of the following:Email address: Enter your password and choose Sign in.  Email or password doesn’t look right? 4. Mobile number: Open the text message that was just sent to your mobile number and enter the 6-digit verification code. Didn’t get a text message? 5. Choose who’s watching and you’re ready to stream.

We didn’t find an account with the email address you entered. Do one of the following: - Check your email address. If you have another email that could be associated with your HBO Max account, choose Edit Email Address and enter a different email address. Need help? Go to Find your account email. - Set up your HBO Max account. If you get HBO Max through an internet, mobile, or TV provider, choose Connect Your Provider, then choose who you get HBO Max (or HBO) through, or type their name in the Search box. Need help? Go to Connect your provider.

To finish signing in, do the following: 1. Check your inbox for an email sent by HBO Max (email subject: Time Sensitive: Your One-Time HBO Max Code). This email should arrive within a few minutes. Be sure to search all inbox folders (including Promotions, Junk, and Spam). 2. Enter the 6-digit code from your email. 3. Add or update an optional password for your account. 4. Choose who’s watching and you’re ready to stream.

Need to switch accounts? To sign in to a different HBO Max account, choose your profile (upper right) and then Sign Out. Once signed out, choose Sign In. Note You can add your mobile number or email to your account as an additional sign-in method. To find out how, go to Change account info. ###### Troubleshooting If you get this message, try signing in without your password. Here’s how: 1. When prompted for your password, choose Get One-Time Code. 2. Check your inbox for an email sent by HBO Max (email subject: Time Sensitive: Your One-Time HBO Max Code). This email should arrive within a few minutes. Be sure to search all inbox folders (including Promotions, Junk, and Spam). Didn’t get the email? 3. Enter the 6-digit code from your email. 4. If prompted, add or update the password for your account. 5. Choose who’s watching and you’re ready to stream.

How are you trying to sign in? If you get this message, try signing in without your password. Here’s how: 1. When prompted for your password, choose Get One-Time Code. 2. Check your inbox for an email sent by HBO Max (email subject: Time Sensitive: Your One-Time HBO Max Code). This email should arrive within a few minutes. Be sure to search all inbox folders (including Promotions, Junk, and Spam). Didn’t get the email? 3. Enter the 6-digit code from your email. 4. If prompted, add or update the password for your account. 5. Choose who’s watching and you’re ready to stream.

If you added your mobile number to your account, here are some things to try: - Make sure the mobile number you entered is correct. You need to add your mobile number to your account before you can sign in with it. - Try signing in with your account email and password. For help finding your account email, go to Find your account email.

If you’re signing in with your mobile number, you should get a new text message after entering your mobile number. If you didn’t get a text message, try the following: - Make sure the mobile number you entered is correct. You need to add your mobile number to your account before you can sign in with it. - Choose Get New Code to send another text message. You should get a new text message within a couple of minutes. - Make sure your phone can receive text messages (check your cell phone signal strength and make sure Airplane mode is turned Off). - Make sure your phone is not set to block messages from unknown senders. - Try restarting your phone.

Account & Sign In: Troubleshoot

Can’t connect HBO Max provider

Can’t connect HBO Max provider

If you get HBO Max through an internet, mobile, or TV provider, you need to connect your provider account (with your HBO Max subscription) before signing in to HBO Max. ##### Already have an account? If you already have an HBO Max account, sign in with your email and password. If you can’t connect your internet, mobile, or TV provider account, find your issue below: If you get this message, make sure HBO Max is included with your internet, mobile, or TV package. To do this, sign in to your internet, mobile, or TV provider account and make sure HBO Max is included. For more info, go to Fix a subscription issue. If you get this message on an Apple device, try removing your TV provider from your iOS or tvOS settings. Here’s how: - iPhone or iPad: Go to Settings > General > TV Provider. Under Allow TV provider to access, turn off HBO Max. - Apple TV: Go to Settings > Users and Accounts > TV Provider. Under Allow TV provider to access, turn off HBO Max.

Now, reopen HBO Max and try the Connect provider steps again. If your provider isn’t on the Connect Your Provider screen, it may be because: - You need to type your provider’s name in the search box, or scroll down through the list of providers (below the company logos). - Disney+, Hulu, HBO Max Bundle or Philo: You need to activate HBO Max (instead of connecting your provider). - You need to sign in with your HBO Max account (instead of connecting your provider). - Your provider may not support HBO Max at this time.

For more help, go to My provider isn’t listed. If you get this message when connecting your provider, it means your provider account is already linked to an HBO Max account. Instead of connecting your provider, try signing in with your HBO Max account email and password. If you’re not sure which email is associated with your HBO Max account, go to Find your account email. Make sure you can sign in to your account on your provider’s website. Here’s how: 1. Go to your internet, mobile, or TV provider’s website. 2. Sign in with your account username and password.Note If you can’t sign in, look for an option to reset your password. If you can’t sign in to your provider account, contact them directly for help. HBO Max support doesn’t have access to your provider sign-in information. 3. After you successfully sign in to your provider’s website, go back to HBO Max and try the Connect provider steps again.

If the connect your provider screen is blank, try the following: 1. On your iPhone or iPad, go to Settings > General > TV Provider. 2. Under Allow TV provider to access, turn off HBO Max. 3. Reopen HBO Max and try the Connect provider steps again.

Additional troubleshooting

If the solutions above didn’t help, choose the device you’re using: You may need to try more than one of these solutions: 1. Delete the HBO Max app from your Apple device. To find out how, go to Apple’s Delete an app article. 2. Open the App Store and install the HBO Max app. 3. Open HBO Max and try the Connect provider steps again.

Try the Connect provider steps on your computer (instead of your iPhone or iPad). Use another browser or try clearing your browsing data. Here’s how: - Safari: On your iOS device, go to Settings > Safari, then scroll down and choose Clear History and Website Data. For more info, go to Clear Safari. - Chrome: Try clearing your Chrome browsing data. To find out how to do this, go to Clear cache & cookies.

Then, open HBO Max and try the Connect provider steps again. You may need to try more than one of these solutions: 1. On your Android device, go to Settings and search for HBO Max. 2. Choose HBO Max from the search results. 3. Choose Storage & cache or Storage. 4. Go back and choose Uninstall. 5. Open the Play Store, search for HBO Max, and choose Install. 6. Open HBO Max and try the Connect provider steps again.

Try the Connect provider steps on your computer (instead of your Android phone or tablet). Use another browser or try clearing your Chrome browsing data on your Android device. To find out how to do this, go to Clear cache & cookies. Then, try the Connect provider steps again. You may need to try more than one of these solutions: 1. On your Amazon Fire tablet, go to Settings > Apps & Games > Manage All Applications. 2. Choose HBO Max from the list of apps. 3. Choose Clear Cache. 4. Choose Clear Data. 5. Choose Uninstall and then OK. 6. Download the HBO Max app from the Amazon Store. 7. Open HBO Max and try the Connect provider steps again.

Try the Connect provider steps on your computer (instead of your Amazon Fire tablet). Try using a different browser or try clearing the browsing data for the browser you’re using. Then, open HBO Max and try the Connect provider steps again. You may need to try more than one of these solutions: - Make sure you’re using a supported browser. - Check for an updated browser version. To find out how to do this, search the internet for update + the name of your browser (i.e. update Chrome).

Try using a different supported browser (Chrome, Firefox, Microsoft Edge, or Safari). This will help determine if the issue is limited to a particular browser. Try the Connect provider steps on your phone or tablet. The connect your provider steps are easier on a phone, tablet, or computer. Try the Connect provider steps on your phone or tablet or on your computer. Once you connect your provider and create an HBO Max account with a password, you can sign in to your HBO Max account on your TV.

Can’t reset HBO Max password

Can’t reset HBO Max password

If you’re having trouble resetting your password, find your problem below: When you reset your password, you should get an HBO Max Password Reset email within a few minutes. If you don’t get a password reset email, try the following: First, search all inbox folders (including Promotions, Junk, and Spam) for an email with the subject: Your HBO Max Password Reset Link. The email is sent by HBO Max (HBOMax@service.hbomax.com). A different email may be associated with your HBO Max account. Try the reset password steps again with another email address of yours. Once you reset your password, sign in to HBO Max with your account email and new password. If you don’t get an HBO Max Password Reset email, try the reset password steps for each of your email accounts. ##### Mistyped email or no longer have access? If your account email has a typo or you no longer have access to your email, please contact us for help. If you’re signed in to HBO Max on a device, here’s how to find your account email: 1. In HBO Max, do one of the following:Phone, tablet, or computer: Choose your profile and then Account. 2. TV device: Choose the Settings icon (image: cog (lower left)) and then Account. 3. Find your account email address.

Now that you know your account email, try the password reset steps again. If you still don’t get a password reset email, please contact us for help. If the reset password link doesn’t work, make sure you open the most recent HBO Max Password Reset email you received. The subject for the password reset email includes the time: Your HBO Max Password Reset Link as of . If this doesn’t help, try using a different browser. Here’s how: 1. On a different computer, phone, or tablet, open the most recent HBO Max Password Reset email that you received. 2. Choose Reset Your Password. 3. Enter a new password and choose Save.

If you still can’t reset your password, please contact us for help. If you get an expired link message, try the following: - Make sure you open the most recent HBO Max Password Reset email you received and choose Reset Your Password. The subject for the password reset email includes the time: Your HBO Max Password Reset Link as of . - Reset your password again

Password reset links expire 24 hours after you request them, or when you request a new password. ##### Can’t access your email? If your account email has a typo or you no longer have access to your email, please contact us for help. Still need help? Please contact us for help.

Forgot my HBO Max email or password

Forgot my HBO Max email or password

If you’re unsure of your account email or password, use the following info to get signed in to HBO Max. Here’s how to sign in without a password: 1. When prompted for your password, choose Get One-Time Code. 2. Check your inbox for an email sent by HBO Max (email subject: Time Sensitive: Your One-Time HBO Max Code). This email should arrive within a few minutes. Be sure to search all inbox folders (including Promotions, Junk, and Spam). Didn’t get the email? 3. Enter the 6-digit code from your email. 4. If prompted, add or update the password for your account. 5. Choose who’s watching and you’re ready to stream.

If you don’t know your HBO Max password, you can reset it. You need to have access to your account email in order to reset your password. If you no longer have access to your email address, please contact us. Here’s how to reset your password: 1. Open HBO Max on your phone or tablet (or go to HBOMax.com on your computer) and choose Sign In. 2. When prompted for your password, choose Forgot Password? 3. Enter your HBO Max account email and choose Submit. Forgot your account email? 4. Check your inbox for an HBO Max Password Reset email. This email should arrive within a few minutes. 5. Open the most recent Password Reset email and choose Reset Your Password. 6. Enter a new password and choose Save.

Now, sign in with your account email and your new password. If you don’t get an HBO Max Password Reset email, go to Can’t reset password. If you don’t know your HBO Max account email, here are a few things to try: - Search all of your email accounts for an email from HBO Max. - Be sure to search all inbox folders (including Promotions, Junk, and Spam).

Once you find the email account associated with HBO Max emails, try signing in with this email address. If you’re signed in on a device, here’s how to find your account email: 1. In HBO Max, do one of the following:Phone, tablet, or computer: Choose your profile and then Account. 2. TV device: Choose the Settings icon (image: cog (lower left)) and then Account. 3. Find your account email address.

Do you have another email address that could be associated with your account? Try the reset password steps with another email address of yours. If there’s an account associated with the email that you entered, you’ll get an HBO Max Password Reset email. If you don’t get this email, try the reset password steps for each of your email accounts. ##### Mistyped email or no longer have access If your account email has a typo or you no longer have access to your email address, please contact us. ##### Troubleshooting ###### Didn’t get the one-time code email? When you sign in without a password, you should get an email with a code within a few minutes. If you didn’t get this email, try the following: First, search all inbox folders (including Promotions, Junk, and Spam) for an email with the subject: Your HBO Max Password Reset Link. The email is sent by HBO Max (HBOMax@service.hbomax.com). A different email may be associated with your HBO Max account. Try signing in with another email address of yours. When you’re prompted for your password, choose Get One-Time Code and follow the Sign in without a password steps. If you’re signed in to HBO Max on a device, here’s how to find your account email: 1. In HBO Max, do one of the following:Phone, tablet, or computer: Choose your profile and then Account. 2. TV device: Choose the Settings icon (image: cog (lower left)) and then Account. 3. Find your account email address. 4. Sign in to HBO Max with this email address. When you’re prompted for your password, choose Get One-Time Code and follow the Sign in without a password steps.

If you added your mobile number to your account, try signing in with your mobile number (instead of your email address).

My provider isn’t listed

Do I need to connect my provider?

It depends on how you get HBO Max (or HBO): - No: If you subscribed through an app store (Amazon, Apple, Google, Roku, Samsung), HBOMax.com, or already activated HBO Max via Philo or the Disney+, Hulu, HBO Max, Bundle, go back and choose Sign In. Then, sign in with your HBO Max email and password. - Yes: If you get HBO Max with your internet, mobile, or TV package, you need to connect your provider** before** signing in to HBO Max.

Billing & Subscription: Billing & Payment

Can I get a refund?

Can I get a refund?

Refund policies vary by billing provider. Your billing provider is who you pay for your HBO Max base plan subscription, add-on, or pay-per-view charge. First, find out who the HBO Max charge is billed through: 1. In HBO Max, do one of the following:Phone, tablet, or computer: Choose your profile and then Subscription. 2. TV device: Choose the Settings icon (image: settings) and then Subscription.(Only Adult profiles can manage subscriptions.) 3. Find who your subscription is billed through (top of screen):Amazon Appstore: Check out Amazon’s Digital products return policy. 4. Apple: Go to Apple’s Request a refund article. 5. Google Play: Go to Google’s Learn about refunds article. 6. Roku: Go to Roku’s refund policy article. 7. Samsung: Contact us with regard to any refund requests. 8. WarnerMedia Direct: There is a no refund policy for subscriptions billed through HBO Max. If there’s an unexpected charge or you have any billing concerns, please contact us for help. 9. Other provider: Contact your billing provider and ask about their refund policy.

To find your billing provider, you can also review your bank statements or ask your internet, mobile, or TV provider if they bill you for HBO Max. Need help signing in? Go to Can’t sign in. ##### What if the charge isn’t what I expected? If your charge looks different than you expected, here are a few possible reasons: - Applicable taxes are added to your subscription charge and they’re based on your billing address ZIP code. - You may have a different plan or billing period than you thought. Which plan do I have? - Your promotional or discounted period may have ended and you were charged the standard subscription rate. - You may have the Extra Member Add-On, which is billed separately from your base plan (if your plan and add-on were purchased on different days). - You may have a pay-per-view charge, which is billed separately from your base plan.

If none of these reasons apply and your subscription is billed through WarnerMedia Direct, please contact us for help (instead of your payment service). If you’re billed through another company, go to Unexpected subscription charge or contact them for help.

Billing & Subscription: Change Subscription

Change a Google-billed subscription

Change a Google-billed subscription

Here’s what to know if your HBO Max subscription is billed through Google. ##### Check if you’re billed through Google To find who bills you for HBO Max, go to Manage an HBO Max subscription. Note As of April 20, 2026, Google billing is no longer available for new or returning subscribers. Any new subscription started on an Android device is billed directly through us. This change doesn’t impact existing Google-billed subscribers. ##### Google billing Q&A Need billing help? Go to Google Play Help. 1. In HBO Max, do one of the following:Phone, tablet, or computer: Choose your profile and then Subscription. 2. TV device: Choose the Settings icon (image: cog (lower left)) and then Subscription.(Only Adult profiles can manage subscriptions.) 3. Find your current plan. Explore HBO Max plans.

To change your Google-billed plan, go to How do I change my plan? Need help signing in? Go to Can’t sign in. Your subscription automatically renews on your next billing date. Here’s how to find your next billing date: 1. Open the Play Store on your Android phone or tablet or go to play.google.com. 2. Choose your profile icon. 3. Choose Payments & subscriptions and then Subscriptions. 4. Find HBO Max in your list of subscriptions and take note of your next payment date.

Things to know
  • Your HBO Max subscription automatically renews until you cancel it.
  • Deleting the HBO Max app does not cancel your subscription.
  • Cancel your subscription at least 24 hours before your next billing date to avoid further charges.

Here’s how to cancel your Google-billed subscription: 1. Open the Play Store app on the device where you started your subscription. 2. Choose your profile icon. 3. Choose Payments & subscriptions and then Subscriptions. 4. Choose HBO Max and then Cancel subscription.

Pause payments

Instead of canceling, you can pause your subscription for up to 3 months. Google won’t bill you and you won’t be able to stream while your subscription is paused. You can continue streaming until your subscription is paused at the end of your current billing period. 1. Go to g.co/play/subscriptions. 2. Make sure you’re signed in to the Google account that has your HBO Max subscription. (To switch accounts, choose your profile icon and then Switch account). 3. Find HBO Max in your list of subscriptions. 4. Choose Manage and then Cancel subscription.

Pause payments

Instead of canceling, you can pause your subscription for up to 3 months. Google won’t bill you and you won’t be able to stream while your subscription is paused. You can continue streaming until your subscription is paused at the end of your current billing period. Once canceled, you should get an email from Google confirming your cancellation. You can continue streaming until your subscription expires at the end of your billing period. You can resubscribe to HBO Max at any time. For more help, go to Google’s Change a Google Play subscription article. Try these things: - Make sure you’re signed in with the same Google account you used to purchase your subscription. - Go to the Android device where you started your HBO Max subscription and view your subscriptions using the steps above. - Check your Google Play order history. To learn more, go to Google’s Order history article.

To find out how to change your payment method, go to Google’s Change payment method article. To find out how to view your billing history, go to Google’s Order history article. Plan switching is no longer available via Google Play. Here’s how to manage your subscription: 1. Go to play.google.com and choose your profile picture (top of screen). 2. Choose Payments & subscriptions, then Subscriptions. 3. Choose HBO Max in your list of subscriptions and then Cancel subscription. Can’t find it? Make sure you’re signed in with the same Google account you used to purchase your subscription. For help, go to Cancel Google-billed subscription. 4. Once your subscription has expired, go to HBOMax.com and resubscribe.

For more info, go to Google’s Change a Google Play subscription article. If you’re getting a ‘Subscription Paused’ message, it may be due to a payment error (or you may have paused your subscription). - Payment error: To resume your subscription, update your payment method. To find out how, go to Google’s Change payment method article. - Paused subscription: To find out how to restart your paused subscription, go to Google’s Change a Google Play subscription article.

Choose a subscription plan and billing period. 4. Follow the on-screen prompts to confirm your new plan change through Roku.

Once your subscription is updated, you should get an email from Roku confirming the change. ###### When will my plan change take effect? The effective date depends on the change: - If you change to a lower-priced plan, your plan change will happen on your next billing date. - If you change to a higher-priced plan, your plan change happens immediately. You’ll be charged the price difference between plans (plus applicable taxes) and your next billing date will be adjusted.

If you’re getting a ‘Subscription Paused’ message, it may be due to a payment error. To resume your subscription, update your Roku payment method. Here’s how: 1. Go to my.roku.com/payment/account and sign in to your Roku account. 2. Update your payment info and choose Save.

  • Cancel your subscription at least 24 hours before your next billing date to avoid further charges.

Here’s how to cancel your Amazon-billed subscription: 1. Sign in to your Amazon account at amazon.com/appstoresubscriptions. 2. Find your HBO Max subscription and turn off auto-renewal.

Once canceled, you should get an email from Amazon confirming your cancellation. You can continue streaming until your subscription expires at the end of your billing period. You can resubscribe to HBO Max at any time. For more help, go to Amazon’s Manage your Appstore subscription article. To change your Amazon payment method, sign in to your Amazon account at amazon.com/cpe/managepaymentmethods. For more info, go to Amazon’s Manage payment methods article. You can change your plan or billing period on your Amazon device. Here’s how: 1. In HBO Max, choose your profile. Can’t sign in? 2. Choose Subscription and then Change Plan.(Only Adult profiles can manage subscriptions.) 3. Choose a subscription plan and billing period. 4. Follow the on-screen prompts to change your plan through Amazon.

Once your subscription is updated, you should get an email from Amazon confirming the change. 1. In HBO Max, choose the Settings icon (image: cog (lower left)) and then Subscription. Can’t sign in? 2. Choose Change Your Plan. 3. Choose a subscription plan and billing period. 4. Follow the on-screen prompts to change your plan through Amazon.

Once your subscription is updated, you should get an email from Amazon confirming the change. ###### When will my plan change take effect? The effective date depends on the change: - If you change to a lower-priced plan, your plan change will happen on your next billing date. - If you change to a higher-priced plan, your plan change happens immediately. Amazon will refund you for the remaining days on your subscription plan and charge you for your new plan. Your next billing date will be adjusted.

Billing & Subscription: Troubleshoot

Fix an HBO Max subscription issue

Fix an HBO Max subscription issue

‘Welcome back’ message

If you’re getting a Welcome back message when you open HBO Max, try the solutions below. Check the status of your HBO Max subscription. Here’s how: 1. Open HBO Max and on the Welcome back screen, do one of the following:Phone or tablet: Choose your profile (upper-right) and then Subscription. 2. Computer: Choose Subscription (top of screen). 3. TV device: Choose the Settings icon (image: cog (lower left)) and then Subscription.-or- 4. Web: Go to HBOMax.com/subscription and sign in. Can’t sign in? 5. Find who your subscription is billed through (top of screen):Amazon Appstore: Sign in at amazon.com/appstoresubscriptions and find your HBO Max subscription status. 6. Amazon Prime Video Channels: Sign in at amazon.com/myac and find your HBO Max subscription status. 7. Apple: Open the Settings app on your iOS device, choose your name, then Subscriptions, and find your HBO Max subscription status. 8. WarnerMedia Direct: If your subscription is active, you’ll find your upcoming payment date. 9. Google Play: Sign in at g.co/play/subscriptions and find your HBO Max subscription status. 10. Roku: Sign in at my.roku.com/account/subscriptions and find your HBO Max subscription status. 11. Samsung TV: Sign in at samsungcheckout.com, then go to Purchase History and choose Subscriptions. 12. Other provider: Sign in to your internet, mobile, or TV provider account to check the status of your HBO Max subscription. If you need help, contact your provider.

If your HBO Max subscription is active, try signing out and back in to HBO Max (the next solution). Try signing out and back in to HBO Max. Here’s how: 1. On the Welcome back screen, do one of the following:Phone or tablet: Choose your profile (upper-right) and then Sign Out. 2. Computer: Choose Sign Out (upper-right). 3. TV device: Choose the Settings icon (image: cog (lower left)) and then Sign Out. 4. Sign in to HBO Max.

If you get HBO Max (or HBO) through an internet, mobile, or TV provider, do the following: 1. Open HBO Max on your phone, tablet, or computer. 2. On the Welcome back screen, choose Connect Your Provider. 3. Choose the company you get HBO Max (or HBO) through. 4. On your provider’s sign-in screen, enter your provider account info and sign in.

Need help? Go to Connect your provider. If you get HBO Max through the Apple App Store, try restoring your app store subscription. 1. Open HBO Max on the Apple device where you started your subscription. 2. Sign out of your account:iPhone or iPad: Choose your profile and then Sign Out. 3. Apple TV: Choose the Settings icon (image: cog (lower left)) and then Sign Out. 4. Choose Sign In. 5. On your Apple TV, choose Use Email Address. 6. On the sign-in screen, choose Restore Purchase. If we find an active HBO Max subscription in the app store, we’ll restore it and sign you into your HBO Max account.

‘Can’t verify your subscription’ message

If you get this message, try the following: - Make sure you have an HBO Max subscription in the Apple App Store. To do this, open the Settings app on your iOS device and tap your name. Then, tap Subscriptions and find HBO Max in your list of subscriptions. - Make sure you’re signed in on your Apple device with the Apple Account that has your HBO Max subscription. - Check your Apple purchase history. To learn more, go to Apple’s Purchase history article.
