Unexpected HBO Max subscription charge

Unexpected HBO Max subscription charge

Find support for an unexpected subscription charge. ##### Charges Q&A Depending on your provider, you can choose from 3 subscription plans: - Basic with Ads: $10.99/month or $109.99/year (16% savings) - Standard: $18.49/month or $184.99/year (16% savings) - Premium: $22.99/month or $229.99/year (16% savings)

If your subscription charge isn’t what you expected, here are some possible reasons: - Applicable taxes are added to your subscription charge based on your billing ZIP code. - You may have a different plan or billing period. Which plan do I have? - You may have the Extra Member Add-On, which is billed separately from your base plan (if your plan and add-on were purchased on different days). - Your promotional or discounted period may have ended and you were charged the standard subscription rate. - You may have a pay-per-view charge, which is billed separately from your base plan.

If none of these reasons apply and your subscription is billed through WarnerMedia Direct, please contact us for help (instead of your payment service). If you’re billed through another company, contact them for help. Find out who bills you. Payment receipts: If your subscription is billed through us, you can get payment receipts at HBOMax.com/subscription. For more info, go to How do I get a payment receipt? If you were charged after canceling your HBO Max subscription, here are some possible reasons: - You canceled your subscription too close to your auto-renewal date. Some providers may require you to cancel at least 24 hours before your next billing period starts (this varies by billing provider). If you have questions, please contact your billing provider. Find out who bills you. - Your subscription is still active. To make sure you’re not charged again, go to Check if your subscription is active. - You have multiple accounts and subscriptions. For help finding your accounts, go to Find multiple accounts.

If you didn’t subscribe to HBO Max, find the HBO Max charge on your bank statement and find who charged you below: - An app store (e.g. Apple or Google): Cancel the HBO Max subscription in the app store or contact their customer support for help. - help.hbomax.com: Take note of the date and charge amount and contact us for help. Be sure to have your payment information and billing ZIP Code handy.

If HBO Max is part of a third-party internet, mobile, or TV package, you may not see an individual charge for HBO Max. If you see more than one charge for HBO Max, there may be a few reasons: Multiple Accounts. If you’re seeing multiple charges each month, you might have multiple accounts (each with an active subscription). For help finding your accounts, go to Find multiple accounts. Authorization hold. If you’re a new HBO Max subscriber, you may see more than one charge for your HBO Max subscription: an authorization hold and the charge for your HBO Max subscription. An authorization hold is done to make sure your payment method is valid and can be successfully charged. An authorization hold is temporary and should be removed within 7-10 days, depending on your financial institution’s policies. Contact your financial institution to find out when the authorization hold will be removed. Note If you purchased a monthly plan and the Extra Member Add-On on different days, they’ll be billed separately each month, resulting in two charges. ##### Troubleshooting Here’s how to find who bills you for HBO Max: 1. Do one of the following:Web: Go to HBOMax.com/subscription and sign in to your account. Can’t sign in? 2. Phone, tablet, or computer: In HBO Max, choose your profile and then Subscription. 3. TV device: In HBO Max, choose the Settings icon (image: (cog, lower left)) and then Subscription. 4. Find who your subscription is billed through (top of screen).

You can also check your bank statements or ask your internet, mobile, or TV provider if they bill you for HBO Max. To find out if your HBO Max subscription is still active, choose your billing provider below: Sign in at HBOMax.com/subscription. If your subscription has been canceled, there will be an option to Resume Your Subscription (instead of Cancel Your Subscription). Sign in at amazon.com/appstoresubscriptions and find your HBO Max subscription. If your subscription has been canceled, there won’t be an auto-renewal date. Sign in at amazon.com/video/subscriptions/manage and find your HBO Max subscription. If your HBO Max subscription has been canceled, there will be a subscription end date. Open the Settings app on your iPhone or iPad. Choose your name and then Subscriptions. If your HBO Max subscription has been canceled, there will be a subscription end date (and there won’t be a cancel option). Sign in to your Google account at g.co/play/subscriptions and find your HBO Max subscription status. Sign in to your Hulu account at hulu.com/account and check if the HBO Max Add-on is listed under Your Subscriptions. Also, choose View Charges to see upcoming charges. Sign in to your Roku account at my.roku.com/subscriptions and find your HBO Max subscription status. Sign in to your Samsung account at samsungcheckout.com. Then, go to Purchase History and choose Subscriptions. YouTube TV: Sign in to your YouTube TV account at https://tv.youtube.com. Choose your profile picture and then Settings > Membership. Choose your profile picture, select Purchases and memberships, and check if HBO Max is listed under Memberships. Sign in to your internet, mobile, or TV provider account and check the status of your HBO Max subscription. If you need help, contact your provider. ##### Cancellation confirmation Some billing providers send you an email when you cancel a subscription. Check your inbox for an email that confirms the cancellation of your HBO Max subscription. Here’s how to find out if you have multiple accounts: 1. Open HBO Max on each device that you use. 2. On each device, do one of the following:Phone, tablet, or computer: Choose your profile and then Account. 3. TV device: Choose the Settings icon (image: cog (lower left)) and then Account. 4. Take note of your account email.

Did you find more than one account email?

Here’s what to do: 1. For each account, do one of the following:Phone, tablet, or computer: Choose your profile and then Subscription. 2. TV device: Choose the Settings icon (image: cog (lower left)) and then Subscription.(Only Adult profiles can manage subscriptions.) 3. Find who your subscription is billed through (top of screen). 4. Decide which subscription you want to cancel and follow the cancellation steps for your billing provider.

If you’re signed in with the same account on all devices and you’re seeing multiple charges each month, contact us for help. When you contact us, be sure to have the following info handy: - Your account email address - Your ZIP Code - Info about charges on your credit card, debit card, PayPal account, or through Apple Pay

Sign Up & Get Started: Get Started

All about HBO Max

All about HBO Max

HBO Max brings all the iconic programming that you love about HBO together with an even deeper library of crave-worthy genres like true crime, documentaries, food, home, and more. Dive into the worlds of HBO, the DC Universe, and Harry Potter—plus hit movies, original series, and the best in true crime, food, comedy, and reality. Discover entertainment for every mood and everyone in the household, all in one place. - HBO series and movies, Max Originals, and select series and movies from Warner Bros., the DC Universe, Cartoon Network, the Turner Library, Looney Tunes, Adult Swim, and more. - The best real-life shows from your favorite TV brands: HGTV, the Food Network, TLC, ID, Magnolia, Discovery, and more.

Find out more at HBOMax.com. HBO Max is currently available in the United States, Latin America, the Caribbean, and in select countries and regions in Asia Pacific and Europe. You can stream HBO Max when you travel to a country or region where HBO Max is available. For more info, go to Where is HBO Max available? You can stream HBO Max on phones, tablets, computers, TVs, streaming players, set-top boxes, and game consoles. For more info, go to Supported devices Here are the HBO Max plans and prices (not including applicable taxes): - Basic with Ads - $10.99/month or $109.99/year (16% savings)Stream in Full HD on 2 devices at once - Standard - $18.49/month or $184.99/year (16% savings)Stream in Full HD on 2 devices at once, up to 30 offline downloads - Premium - $22.99/month or $229.99/year (16% savings)Stream in 4K UHD on 4 devices at once, up to 100 offline downloads

Find out more at HBO Max plans. *May include restrictions in some content categories. The payment methods vary depending on who you subscribe through: - HBOMax.com: You can use any of the following: Visa, MasterCard, Discover, American Express, JCB, PayPal, and Apple Pay. Please note that your credit or debit card needs to be U.S. issued and support automatic recurring payments. - Internet, mobile, or TV provider: Check with your provider to find out which payment methods are accepted. - App store: Check with your app store to find out which payment methods are accepted.

Some colleges and universities include access to HBO Max. To find out if your college or university offers HBO Max, go to HBO Max providers. Student discount: A discounted HBO Max plan is available to students (age 18+) whose U.S. accredited college or university enrollment status has been verified by UNiDAYS. With the student discount you can get the Basic with Ads plan for $5.49/month (a 50% savings) for up to 12 months. For more info, go to HBO Max student discount. At this time we don’t have HBO Max gift cards. Before you sign up, find out if you already have access to HBO Max. If you don’t already have access, you can sign up at HBOMax.com or through one of our partners. For more info, go to How do I subscribe to HBO Max?

HBO Max student discount

HBO Max student discount

An HBO Max student discount is available to students (age 18+) whose U.S. accredited college or university enrollment status has been verified by UNiDAYS. With the student discount you can get the HBO Max Basic with Ads plan for $5.49/month (a 50% savings) for up to 12 months. You need to join UNiDAYS and create a free UNiDAYS account to get the HBO Max student discount. - Basic with Ads monthly plan $5.49/month (50% savings) for 12 months

Sign up for HBO Max student discount

Already have an HBO Max subscription? To avoid being billed for multiple subscriptions, manage your HBO Max subscription before signing up for the HBO Max student discount. Here’s how to get the HBO Max student discount through UNiDAYS: 1. Go to the UNiDAYS website at myunidays.com/account/log-in. 2. Do one of the following:Already have a UNiDAYS account? Enter your account email and password and choose Log in. 3. New to UNiDAYS? Choose Join now to create a free account and verify your student status. 4. On the UNiDAYS website, find the HBO Max offer (search for ‘HBO Max’ or browse the Entertainment offers). 5. Select the HBO Max offer and choose Get now > Reveal code.You’ll get a promo code that you can redeem to get the student discount. 6. Choose Copy to copy the promo code, and then choose Launch website.You’ll be taken to HBOMax.com/redeem to redeem your code. 7. Paste the code and choose Apply.For help, go to Troubleshoot promo code. 8. When prompted to create your HBO Max account, do one of the following:New subscribers: Complete the account form and choose Create Account. Add your payment method and choose Start Subscription. 9. Returning subscribers: Enter your HBO Max email address and choose Sign In. Once you’re signed in, choose Confirm Change.

Now, you can sign in and stream HBO Max on all your favorite devices. Only the Basic with Ads monthly plan is available through UNiDAYS. To keep your HBO Max student discount, UNiDAYS will ask you to verify your student status each year. Please note that if you change your HBO Max plan, your student discount will not apply to your new plan (the discount will end when your new plan takes effect). Need help? If you need help signing up for the HBO Max student offer, please contact UNiDAYS support. ##### Reverify your student status If you get an email with the subject line FYI: Your student discount expires soon, follow the link in the email to reverify your student status with UNiDAYS. You can also reverify your student status through UNiDAYS. To do this, sign in at myunidays.com and follow the prompts to reverify your student status. For more info, go to myunidays.com/support/my-account/still-a-student. Need help? If you need help reverifying your student status with UNiDAYS, contact UNiDAYS support.

HBO Max through DoorDash

HBO Max through DoorDash

The HBO Max Basic with Ads plan is no longer included with the DoorDash DashPass Annual plan. If you’ve already activated HBO Max, you can continue streaming until your DashPass annual billing cycle ends. When your current DoorDash DashPass Annual plan expires or renews, your HBO Max access will end and you’ll need to resubscribe. ##### How do I cancel my HBO Max subscription? You can stream HBO Max Basic with Ads until your DashPass Annual Plan billing cycle ends. When your current DoorDash DashPass Annual plan expires or renews your HBO Max access will end. To continue streaming, you’ll need to resubscribe. Here’s how to cancel your HBO Max Standard plan billed through DoorDash: 1. Go to doordash.com and sign in to your DoorDash account. 2. Choose Account (lower-left corner), then Manage DashPass. 3. Choose Manage HBO Max Subscription, then Cancel Subscription.

Once canceled, you can continue streaming HBO Max Standard until your subscription expires at the end of your monthly billing period. After your HBO Max Standard subscription ends, you can stream HBO Max Basic with Ads until your DashPass Annual Plan billing cycle ends.

How do I subscribe to HBO Max?

How do I subscribe to HBO Max?

Things to know
  • Find out if you already have access to HBO Max (e.g., HBO subscribers).
  • You can sign up on your device or through a provider.
Sign up on your device

Choose how you’d like to sign up: Here’s how to subscribe to HBO Max: 1. Go to HBOMax.com/product. 2. Choose a bundle or plan, then continue. 3. On the Get Started screen, enter an email address for your HBO Max account (if you already have an HBO Max account, enter your account email). 4. Do one of the following:New subscribers: Complete the account form and choose Create Account. You’ll sign in with this info. Check your email and password message? 5. Returning subscribers: Enter your HBO Max password and choose Sign In.Can’t sign in? 6. Add your payment method and start your subscription. 7. Choose Start Streaming and you’re ready to watch HBO Max.

Now you can sign in and stream HBO Max on any supported device. Your subscription will auto-renew on a recurring basis until you cancel. Need help? Go to Troubleshooting. ##### Personalize your profile The first time you use a new Adult profile, you can personalize your experience by choosing your favorite genres, brands, shows, and movies. To get started, choose Let’s Go. If you don’t want to share your preferences, choose Skip to start streaming. You can update your favorites on your phone, tablet, or computer by choosing the preference selector when you edit a profile. Here’s how to subscribe to HBO Max: 1. Open HBO Max and choose Subscribe Now. 2. Choose a subscription plan and billing period, then continue. 3. On the Get Started screen, enter an email address for your HBO Max account (if you already have an HBO Max account, enter your account email). 4. Do one of the following:New subscribers: Complete the account form and choose Create Account. You’ll sign in with this info. Check your email and password message? 5. Returning subscribers: Enter your HBO Max password and choose Sign In.Can’t sign in? 6. Follow the on-screen instructions to subscribe. 7. Choose Start Streaming and you’re ready to watch HBO Max.

Where is HBO Max available?

Where is HBO Max available?

Here’s where HBO Max is currently available: HBO Max is currently available in these countries and regions: - Australia, Bangladesh, Bhutan, Brunei, Cambodia, Cook Islands, Fiji, Hong Kong, Indonesia, Kiribati, Laos, Macau, Malaysia, Maldives, Marshall Islands, Micronesia, Mongolia, Myanmar, Nauru, Nepal, New Zealand, Niue, Pakistan, Palau, Papua New Guinea, Philippines, Samoa, Singapore, Solomon Islands, Sri Lanka, Taiwan, Thailand, Timor-Leste, Tokelau, Tonga, Tuvalu, Vanuatu, and Vietnam

HBO Max is currently available in these countries and territories: - Albania, Andorra, Armenia, Austria, Belgium, Bosnia and Herzegovina, Bulgaria, Croatia, Cyprus, Czech Republic, Denmark, Estonia, Finland, France, Georgia, Germany, Greece, Hungary, Iceland, Ireland, Italy, Kazakhstan, Kyrgyzstan, Latvia, Liechtenstein, Lithuania, Luxembourg, Malta, Moldova, Montenegro, Netherlands, North Macedonia, Norway, Poland, Portugal, Romania, Serbia, Slovakia, Slovenia, Spain, Sweden, Switzerland, Tajikistan, Türkiye, Ukraine, and United Kingdom

HBO Max is currently available in these countries and territories: - Latin America: Argentina, Belize, Bolivia, Brazil, Chile, Colombia, Costa Rica, Ecuador, El Salvador, Guatemala, Guyana, Honduras, Mexico, Nicaragua, Panama, Paraguay, Peru, Suriname, Uruguay, Venezuela - Caribbean: Anguilla, Antigua & Barbuda, Aruba, Bahamas, Barbados, British Virgin Islands, Cayman Islands, Curacao, Dominica, Dominican Republic, Grenada, Haiti, Jamaica, Montserrat, Saint Kitts and Nevis, Saint Lucia, St. Vincent and the Grenadines, Trinidad & Tobago, Turks and Caicos

HBO Max is currently available in these countries and territories: - The United States (including American Samoa, Guam, the Northern Mariana Islands, Puerto Rico, and the US Virgin Islands)

HBO Max availability Q&A

We anticipate that HBO Max will be available in additional countries and regions in 2026 and beyond. Stay tuned for more info. You can stream HBO Max when you travel to a country or region where HBO Max is available. You can also play shows and movies that you’ve already downloaded. Your home country is the country where you signed up for HBO Max. When you travel to a country where HBO Max is available: - Shows and movies: What’s available to stream will change based on your physical location. As a result, you may not be able to stream some titles on your Continue Watching and My List when you travel to another country. - Ratings: Content ratings are based on your physical location. If you travel to a country with a different content rating system, the parental controls that you set are applied, however, shows and movies may be rated differently. - Downloads: You can play shows and movies that you’ve already downloaded on your phone or tablet.

If you travel to a country or region where HBO Max is not available, you won’t be able to stream. You can, however, still play shows and movies that you’ve already downloaded. Before you travel to another country - Download shows and movies on your phone or tablet. - Make sure you’re signed in to HBO Max on the device with your downloads.

Now, you can watch your downloads when you travel to any country or region. ##### Traveling in the US If you’re traveling within the U.S. and get the message ‘Subscribe today to keep streaming,’ select I’m Traveling to continue watching HBO Max. For more info, go to HBO Max account sharing. ##### Troubleshooting If you’re getting a ‘This is Uncharted Territory’ or ‘Not available in your region yet’ message, your IP address might be blocked or is incorrectly identifying your location. If HBO Max is available where you’re located, try these steps: Disable your VPN, proxy, or anonymization service so that your IP address is not blocked. Try restarting your modem and router. Here’s how: 1. Turn off or unplug the device with HBO Max. 2. Unplug the power from your network devices (modem, router, etc.). 3. Wait 30 seconds. 4. Reconnect the power to your modem and wait for it to connect to the internet (about a minute). 5. Reconnect the power to your router and wait for it to connect to the internet. 6. Turn your device back on, open HBO Max, and try watching something.

If you’ve disabled VPNs, proxies, and anonymization services, please contact your Internet Service Provider for help. If these steps don’t help, gather the following info and contact us. - Your Internet Service Provider (ISP) - Your IP address (open a browser and type ‘what is my IP address’ to get this)

Sign Up & Get Started: Setup

AirPlay HBO Max to your TV

AirPlay HBO Max to your TV

You can use AirPlay to stream HBO Max from your iPhone, iPad, or Mac to your AirPlay 2-compatible device. Note The HBO Max app is the easiest way to stream HBO Max on your TV. To find out if there’s an HBO Max app available, go to Supported devices (or check the app store on your TV device). ##### Stream HBO Max to your TV using AirPlay You can stream HBO Max from your iPhone or iPad to your AirPlay 2-compatible device. Here’s how: 1. Connect your iPhone or iPad to the same Wi-Fi network as your AirPlay 2-compatible device. 2. Make sure AirPlay is enabled on your AirPlay 2-compatible device. 3. Open HBO Max on your iPhone or iPad and start watching something. 4. Tap the screen and then choose the AirPlay icon (image: rectangle and triangle). 5. Choose the device that you want to AirPlay to.

Need help? Go to Apple’s AirPlay isn’t working article. ##### Mirror your screen on your TV You can mirror your iPhone, iPad, or Mac screen on your AirPlay 2-compatible device. Here’s how: 1. Connect your iPhone, iPad, or Mac to the same Wi-Fi network as your AirPlay 2-compatible device. 2. Make sure AirPlay is enabled on your AirPlay 2-compatible device. 3. Do one of the following:iPhone or iPad: Open the Control Center and choose Screen Mirroring. Then, choose the device that you want to AirPlay to. For help, go to Apple’s Mirror your iOS screen article. 4. Mac: Choose the Control Center icon in the menu bar (top right) and then Screen Mirroring. Then, choose the device that you want to AirPlay to. For help, go to Apple’s Stream video and audio with AirPlay article. 5. If asked, enter the four-digit passcode on your iOS device or Mac.

Chromecast HBO Max to your TV

Chromecast HBO Max to your TV

You can use Chromecast to cast HBO Max from your phone, tablet, or computer to your TV. ##### What you need - Chromecast (2nd or 3rd gen), Chromecast Ultra, or a TV with Chromecast built-in(If you have a Chromecast with Google TV, you can use the HBO Max app.) - An Android phone or tablet, an Apple iPhone or iPad, or a computer with the Chrome browser

The HBO Max app is the easiest way to stream HBO Max on your TV. Check the app store on your TV or go to Supported devices. ##### Which device are you casting from? Here’s how to cast HBO Max from your phone or tablet to your TV: 1. Make sure the phone or tablet is connected to the same Wi-Fi network as your Chromecast. 2. Open HBO Max and choose the Google Cast button (image: rectangle with Wi-Fi symbol).Cast button missing? Check the Troubleshooting section below. 3. Choose the device that you want to cast to. 4. Start watching something.

To stop casting, choose the Cast button again and then Disconnect. You can change the subtitles, closed captions, and the audio track on the device you’re using to cast. For more info, go to Subtitles, captions, and audio. Here’s how to cast HBO Max from your computer to your TV: 1. Make sure your computer is connected to the same Wi-Fi network as your Chromecast. 2. Open the Chrome browser on your computer and sign in at HBOMax.com. 3. Click the Google Cast button (image: rectangle with Wi-Fi symbol).Cast button missing? Go to Google’s Missing the Cast button? article. 4. Choose the device that you want to cast to. 5. Start watching something.

To stop casting, choose the Cast button again and then Disconnect. You can change the subtitles, closed captions, and the audio track on the device you’re using to cast. For more info, go to Subtitles, captions, and audio. For more help, go to Google’s Cast from Chrome to your TV article. ##### Troubleshooting If the Google Cast button (image: rectangle with Wi-Fi symbol) is missing, you may need to adjust your iOS settings. Here’s how: 1. On your iPhone or iPad, go to Settings, then scroll down and select HBO Max. 2. Make sure Bluetooth and Local Network are both turned on. 3. Close HBO Max and reopen the app.

It may take a minute for the Cast button to appear. Try casting from the Google Home app. Here’s how: 1. Open the Google Home app on your phone or tablet. 2. Choose the device that you want to cast to. 3. Choose Cast my screen > Cast screen. 4. Once your screen appears on your TV, open HBO Max and start streaming.

If you’re using a Chromecast device, try the following troubleshooting: - Check that your TV is set to display the same numbered HDMI input that your Chromecast device is connected to. - Unplug your Chromecast from power and the HDMI port, wait a minute, then plug it back in. - Try connecting your Chromecast to a different HDMI port on your TV.

It may take a minute for the Cast button to appear. Note 1st Gen Chromecast devices are not supported. For more info, go to Chromecast Help: - Chromecast troubleshooter - Cast your Android screen from Google Home - Improve your Android screen casting experience

HBO Max settings

HBO Max settings

There are a number of settings available in the HBO Max app and at HBOMax.com/settings. ##### Choose the device you’re using: To access settings: - In HBO Max, choose your profile and then App Settings.

Here you’ll find the following options: You can stream over Wi-Fi or your cellular network using mobile data. To limit your streaming to a Wi-Fi connection: - Android: Turn on Stream over Wi-Fi only. - iOS: Turn off Stream over Cellular.

By default, the next episode in a series automatically plays when you’re done watching an episode, so you can easily watch a series. If you don’t want to autoplay the next episode, turn off Autoplay Next Episode. ###### How autoplay works When the credits begin, Next Episode appears in the lower right with a 15-second countdown. To start watching right away, choose Next Episode or wait 15 seconds and the episode will automatically play. To watch the end credits, choose the X beside the Next Episode button to dismiss the countdown. If you do this, the next episode won’t play until the episode is completely done (including scenes from the next episode). By default, movie and series recommendations play when you’re done watching something. If you don’t want recommendations, turn off Autoplay Recommended Movies & Series. Here you’ll find how much storage space is available on your phone or tablet. - Download Over Wi-Fi Only: Turn this option on to only allow downloads when connected to a Wi-Fi network (prevents downloads over your cellular network). - Video Quality: You can choose from three different video quality levels: Good, Better, or Best (the default setting is Good).

To view, renew, or delete downloads on your device, choose Manage Downloads. For more info, go to Download shows and movies. Here you’ll find the following options: - App Notifications: If you don’t want pop-up notifications about new HBO Max shows and movies, plus other suggestions and notices, choose App Notifications and then turn HBO Max notifications off in your device settings. - Emails from HBO Max and Affiliates: We periodically email you show and movie recommendations, product updates, and special offers. If you don’t want to receive these emails, turn off this option.

If you turn off Emails from HBO Max, you’ll still get service emails when you make changes to your account (such as changing your password or signing in on a new device). Install HBO Max on supported devices

Install HBO Max on supported devices

You can stream HBO Max on phones, tablets, computers, TVs, streaming players, set-top boxes, and game consoles. To start streaming today, install the HBO Max app or go to HBOMax.com on your computer. ##### Choose a device type: Choose a supported device for install and update steps: - Amazon Fire tablets (with Fire OS 6 or later)

Install: On your Amazon Fire tablet, choose Apps, and search for HBO Max. Choose HBO Max and then the download option. Update: On your Amazon Fire tablet, choose Apps, and search for HBO Max. If an update is available, choose Update. - Most Android phones and tablets (with Android OS 7.1 or later)

Install: On your Android phone or tablet, open the Play Store and search for HBO Max. Choose HBO Max and then the download option. Installing HBO Max from somewhere other than the official Google Play app store is not supported. Update: On your Android phone or tablet, open the Play Store and search for HBO Max. If an update is available, choose Update. The HBO Max mobile app is not currently supported on Google Chromebooks. To stream on Google Chromebooks, go to HBOMax.com with the Chrome browser. (image: Get it on Google Play) - Apple iPhone, iPad, and iPod Touch (with iOS 16.4 or later)

Install: On your Apple device, open the App Store and search for HBO Max. Choose HBO Max and then the download option. Update: On your Apple device, open the App Store and search for HBO Max. If an app update is available, choose Update. (image: Download on the App Store) ###### Subscribe or sign in Once you have the HBO Max app installed, do one of the following: - New subscriber? Stream HBO Max with an HDMI cable

Stream HBO Max with an HDMI cable

The HBO Max app is the easiest way to stream HBO Max on your TV. To find out if there’s an HBO Max app available, go to Supported devices (or check the app store on your TV device). Here are some other ways to stream on your TV: - AirPlay - Chromecast - HDMI cable

Stream using an HDMI cable

You can use an HDMI cable to connect your computer to your TV. ###### What you need - An HDMI 2.0+ cable and possibly an adapter(e.g., Mac: Apple USB-C Digital AV Adapter) - A TV that supports HDCP 2.1+

To stream on your TV using an HDMI cable: 1. Connect an HDMI cable to your computer (an adapter may be needed). 2. Connect the other end of your HDMI cable to your TV. 3. Open HBO Max on your computer and start watching something.

Troubleshooting

If you’re getting this message, go to There’s a problem with your device setup. Here are some things to try: - Disconnect and reconnect both ends of your HDMI cable. If you’re using an adapter, make sure it’s securely connected to your HDMI cable. - Try using another HDMI cable. - Try reversing the ends of your HDMI cable. - Try using another HDMI port on your TV. - Make sure your TV is set to the correct HDMI source.

Here are some things to try: - Try changing the audio source on your TV or audio receiver. - Try using another HDMI port on your TV or receiver. - Try using another HDMI cable.

For more help, go to HBO Max audio issues.

HBO Max free trial Q&A

HBO Max free trial Q&A

Frequently asked questions and answers: Most HBO Max subscription providers do not offer a free trial to new subscribers. If a subscription plan or promotional offer includes a free trial, you’ll see mention of the free trial when you sign up. During a free trial, you can stream anything on the HBO Max subscription plan with the free trial. If you enjoy HBO Max during your free trial, you don’t have to take any action. After your free trial ends, your subscription will automatically renew each billing period until you cancel it. If a subscription plan or promotional offer includes a free trial, the eligibility criteria is mentioned when you sign up. For example, some promotional offers are only available to new HBO Max subscribers. If you think you were incorrectly billed, reach out to your billing provider for help (go to Who’s billing me?). If a subscription plan or promotional offer includes a free trial, the trial length is mentioned when you sign up. When you start your free trial, you need to add a payment method during sign up so your subscription can automatically convert to a paid subscription after your trial ends. Your first charge is typically after your free trial is over (this can vary depending on your billing provider). After your free trial, your subscription will automatically renew each billing period until you cancel it. To avoid being charged for your next billing period, you need to cancel your subscription before your next billing date. Billing questions? Go to HBO Max plans and billing. If you’re charged after starting a free trial, here are a few possible reasons: - Offer doesn’t include a free trial. If your subscription plan or promotional offer doesn’t include a free trial, you’ll be charged for HBO Max at the time you sign up. - Not eligible for a free trial. If you don’t meet the eligibility criteria for the subscription plan or promotional offer with a free trial, you’ll be charged for HBO Max at the time of sign up. For example, some promotional offers are only available to new subscribers. If you think you were incorrectly billed, reach out to your billing provider for help (see Who’s billing me? below). - Authorization hold. The pending charge is likely an authorization hold. An authorization hold is done to make sure your payment method is valid and can be successfully charged in the future (once your free trial is over).An authorization hold is temporary and should be removed within 7-10 days, depending on your financial institution’s policies. The authorization hold is not a charge, but may affect your available balance. You can contact your financial institution to find out when the authorization hold will be removed, and when the hold amount will be added back to your available balance.

Here’s how to find who bills you for HBO Max: 1. Do one of the following:Web: Go to HBOMax.com/subscription and sign in to your account. Can’t sign in? 2. Phone, tablet, or computer: In HBO Max, choose your profile and then Subscription. 3. TV device: In HBO Max, choose the Settings icon (image: (cog, lower left)) and then Subscription. 4. Here you’ll find who your subscription is billed through (top of screen).

You can also check your bank statements or ask your internet, mobile, or TV provider if they bill you for HBO Max. (If your subscription is part of a TV package or mobile plan, you won’t see an individual charge for HBO Max.) Billing questions? Go to HBO Max plans and billing. To avoid being charged for your next billing period, you need to cancel your subscription before your free trial ends. If you cancel early, you can continue streaming until your trial ends. - How do I get HBO Max? - What plans does HBO Max offer? - Which devices work with HBO Max?

If you get this message, try the following: - To change your plan, you need to be located in the country where you started your subscription. If you’re traveling, wait until you return home to change your subscription plan. - Try changing your plan again in a few minutes.

If you still can’t change your plan, find out who your subscription is billed through and contact them for help. Who’s billing me?

Watch HBO Max: Troubleshoot

‘Are you using a VPN?’ message

‘Are you using a VPN?’ message

This message means the device you’re using appears to be connected to a VPN (Virtual Private Network). To stream HBO Max, you need to turn off your VPN, proxy, or anonymization service. If you’re getting the ‘Are you using a VPN’ message, try the following: Turn off your VPN, proxy, or anonymization service so that your IP address reflects your current location. To find out how to do this, contact your VPN provider, internet service provider, or your network administrator. Once you’ve turned off your VPN or proxy, try streaming again. Next, try restarting your modem and router. Here’s how: 1. Turn off or unplug the device with HBO Max. 2. Unplug the power from your network devices (modem, router, etc.). 3. Wait 30 seconds. 4. Reconnect the power to your modem and wait for it to connect to the internet (about a minute). 5. Reconnect the power to your router and wait for it to connect to the internet. 6. Turn your device back on, open HBO Max, and try streaming again.

If you’re using a computer, try streaming on another device. If you don’t get a VPN message on another device, try the following: - Try using a different supported browser (Chrome, Edge, Firefox, or Safari).

-or- - Try clearing your browser cookies. To find out how, go to Troubleshoot HBOMax.com and choose your browser.

Open a browser window and type ‘what is my IP address’ in the search box. Here you’ll find location information and your Internet Service Provider (ISP). If you’ve tried the previous steps and you’re still getting VPN message, please contact your Internet Service Provider for help. If you’re still getting the VPN message, gather the following info and contact us. - Your Internet Service Provider (ISP) - Your IP address (open a browser and type ‘what is my IP address’ to get this)

‘There’s a problem with your device setup’ message

‘There’s a problem with your device setup’ message

If you’re getting the ‘There’s a problem with your device setup’ message, try the solutions below. ##### Choose the device you are using: You may need to try more than one of these solutions. ##### Installing HBO Max Installing HBO Max from somewhere other than an official app store (e.g., Google Play Store, Apple App Store, or Amazon Appstore) is not supported. For more info, go to Install HBO Max on supported devices. - Android: Open the Play Store on your Android device and search for HBO Max. If an app update is available, choose Update. - iOS: Open the App Store on your Apple device and search for HBO Max. If an app update is available, choose Update. - Amazon Fire tablet: Open the Amazon Appstore on your Fire tablet and search for HBO Max. If an app update is available, choose Update.

Once updated, try using HBO Max again. - Android: Open Settings, then choose System > Software Update (steps vary based on your Android version). - iOS: Open Settings, then choose General > Software Update. - Amazon Fire tablet: Open Settings, then choose Device Options > System Updates.

Once updated, try using HBO Max again. Some third-party apps may prevent you from streaming HBO Max. Here are some types of apps that might affect HBO Max: - Ad blockers - IP masking - Screen capture

Exit third-party apps on your phone or tablet and then try streaming HBO Max again. Restart your phone or tablet and network devices (if connected to a Wi-Fi network). Here’s how: 1. Turn off your phone or tablet with HBO Max. 2. Unplug the power from your network devices (modem, router, etc.). 3. Wait 30 seconds. 4. Reconnect the power to your modem and wait for it to connect to the internet (about 1 minute). 5. Reconnect the power to your router and wait for it to connect to the internet. 6. Turn on your phone or tablet, open HBO Max, and try watching something.

Delete the HBO Max app from your phone or tablet and install it again from the app store. Then open HBO Max, sign in, and try using the app again. You may need to try more than one of these solutions. You can stream on your PC, Mac, or Google Chromebook at HBOMax.com. Try using the latest version of a supported browser: - Google Chrome, Firefox, Microsoft Edge for Windows, or Safari

To find out how to update your browser, go to Troubleshoot HBOMax.com. Try using a different supported browser (Chrome, Firefox, Microsoft Edge, or Safari). If you don’t get the device setup message with a different browser, check your browser configuration: - Make sure third-party cookies are enabled - Disable ad-blocking software - Turn off browser extensions, plug-ins, or add-ons - Clear cache and cookies

For help with your browser, go to Troubleshoot HBOMax.com. If you’re connecting your computer/laptop to your TV, here are some things to try: - Unplug your TV, wait a minute, and then plug it back in again. Then, open HBO Max and try watching something. - Make sure your HDMI cable is HDMI 2.0+ compliant. - Disconnect and reconnect both ends of your HDMI cable. - Try using another HDMI cable. - Try using another HDMI port on your TV.

Multiple computer monitors? If your computer has multiple monitors, try streaming HBO Max on your primary monitor. If you still get the device setup message, make sure your adapters, graphics card, software, drivers, video cables, and monitors are all HDCP 2.1+ compliant. Some third-party software may prevent you from streaming HBO Max. Here are some types of software that might affect HBO Max: - Ad blockers - IP masking software - Screen capture software

Disable third-party software running on your computer and then try streaming HBO Max again. Restart your computer and network devices. Here’s how: 1. Close all apps and restart your computer. 2. Unplug the power from your network devices (e.g., modem, router, etc.). 3. Wait 30 seconds. 4. Reconnect the power to your modem and wait for it to connect to the internet (about 1 minute). 5. Reconnect the power to your router and wait for it to connect to the internet. 6. Turn on your computer, go to HBOMax.com, and try watching something.

You may need to try more than one of these solutions. ##### Installing HBO Max Installing HBO Max from somewhere other than an official app store (e.g., Google Play Store or Amazon Appstore) is not supported. For more info, go to Install HBO Max on supported devices. Make sure the HBO Max app and your device software are up-to-date. To find out how to check for updates, go to Troubleshoot HBOMax.com. If you’re using a streaming player (e.g., Roku) connected to your TV, here are some things to try: - Unplug your TV and streaming player, wait a minute, and then plug it back in again. Then, open HBO Max and try watching something. - Make sure your HDMI cable is HDMI 2.0+ compliant. - Disconnect and reconnect both ends of your HDMI cable. - Try using another HDMI cable. - Try reversing the ends of your HDMI cable. - Try using another HDMI port on your TV. - Bypass your receiver or stereo equipment and connect your streaming player directly to your TV.

If you still get the device setup message, make sure all components of your device setup (including set-top boxes, cables, adapters, TV or monitor, and audio equipment) are HDCP 2.1+ compliant (you’ll need HDCP 2.2 to play titles in 4K UHD). For help with your device, check your device documentation or contact support. Some third-party apps may cause issues while streaming HBO Max. Here are some types of apps that might affect HBO Max: - Ad blockers - IP masking - Screen capture

Exit third-party apps on your TV and then try streaming HBO Max again. Restart your TV, streaming player, and network devices. Here’s how: 1. Unplug your TV device and streaming player (if applicable). 2. Unplug the power from your network devices (e.g., modem, router, etc.). 3. Wait 30 seconds. 4. Reconnect the power to your modem and wait for it to connect to the internet (about 1 minute). 5. Reconnect the power to your router and wait for it to connect to the internet. 6. Plug your TV device and streaming player back in, open HBO Max, and try watching something.

If possible, delete the HBO Max app from your TV device and install it again from the app store. Then open HBO Max, sign in, and try using the app again. For help deleting the HBO Max app, go to Troubleshoot the HBO Max app. Still need help? Let us know what’s happening and what steps you’ve tried when you contact us.

HBO Max audio issues

HBO Max audio issues

Audio for shows and movies on HBO Max is available in stereo, 5.1 surround sound, or Dolby Atmos. HBO Max automatically plays the highest quality audio available for your device and subscription plan. To change the audio language, go to Subtitles, captions, and audio. ##### Dolby Q&A You can watch select titles with Dolby Atmos audio on select devices with the Premium plan. To find out if a title is available in Dolby Atmos, choose the title in HBO Max and look for Dolby Atmos on the description screen. ###### Supported Dolby Atmos devices All devices must support HDMI 2.0 and HDCP 2.2 (your TV, streaming device, audio/video receiver, cables, adapters, and any other connected devices). - Amazon Fire TV - Android TVs - Apple TV 4K - AirPlay 2-compatible 4K TVs - Cox Contour 2 and Contour Stream Player - Hisense VIDAA TVs - 4K LG Smart TVs - iPhone and iPad - PlayStation 5 - Roku Ultra, Roku Ultra LT, Roku Streaming Stick 4K, Roku Streaming Stick+, Roku Express 4K, Roku Premiere, Roku 4K TVs, Roku 8K TVs, Roku 4 - Samsung 4K TVs - Sharp TiVo 4K TVs (2025 models and later) - VIZIO 4K Smart TVs - Xbox console - Xfinity X1 (Xi6) and Flex

Dolby Digital Plus audio is available on the following devices: - Amazon Fire TV - Android TV - Apple TV - Google Chromecast Ultra - Hisense VIDAA TVs - LG Smart TV - Roku - Samsung TV (models that support Dolby Digital Plus) - VIZIO Smart TV - Xbox console

Troubleshooting

Before you begin, try playing a different show or movie. If the sound issue is limited to a particular show or movie, please contact us and let us know. If you’re still having audio issues, choose your device below: Here are some things to try: - Turn up the volume using the volume up button on your phone or tablet. - Plug in headphones to check if there’s sound. - Try turning off Bluetooth on your device to make sure your device isn’t sending audio to a Bluetooth accessory (in Settings on your Android or iOS device). - Make sure the Do Not Disturb option on your device isn’t turned on. - Try restarting your phone or tablet.

Here are some things to try: - Check the volume control in HBO Max on your computer to make sure the volume isn’t muted or set low. - If you’re using external speakers, check your speaker cables and the volume control. - Check your computer’s audio output device. - Update your browser to the latest version. To find out how to do this, go to Troubleshoot HBO Max. - Try another supported device.

Try switching the audio setting on your device to stereo sound. To do this, on your Amazon Fire TV go to Settings > Display & Sounds > Audio > Dolby Digital Output and then choose Dolby Digital Plus OFF. More things to try: - Update the HBO Max app. To do this, open the app store on your device and search for HBO Max. If there’s an app update available, choose update or download. For more help, find your device on the Troubleshoot HBO Max page. - Restart your device. Unplug your streaming device (e.g., Roku), wait a minute, and plug it back in again. - Disconnect and reconnect both ends of your HDMI cable. - Try reversing the ends of your HDMI/optical cable or try using a different cable. - Try using another HDMI port on your TV or receiver. - Make sure your TV monitor and sound system also support Dolby Atmos audio.

Note For Dolby Atmos audio, all devices must support HDMI 2.0 and HDCP 2.2 (your TV, streaming device, audio/video receiver, cables, adapters, and any other connected devices). By default, Apple TV uses the best audio format available. If surround sound isn’t working, try changing the audio format to Dolby Digital 5.1. To do this, on your Apple TV go to Settings > Video and Audio > Audio Format and turn on Change Format. Here you can choose Dolby Digital 5.1 (instead of Use Best Available). If changing the audio format to Dolby Digital 5.1 doesn’t help, try changing the Audio Format to Stereo. For more info about audio settings, go to Apple’s Play audio in Dolby Atmos or surround sound article. More things to try: - Update the HBO Max app. To do this, open the app store on your device and search for HBO Max. If there’s an app update available, choose update or download. For more help, find your device on the Troubleshoot HBO Max page. - Restart your device. Unplug your streaming device (e.g., Roku), wait a minute, and plug it back in again. - Disconnect and reconnect both ends of your HDMI cable. - Try reversing the ends of your HDMI/optical cable or try using a different cable. - Try using another HDMI port on your TV or receiver. - Make sure your TV monitor and sound system also support Dolby Atmos audio.

Note For Dolby Atmos audio, all devices must support HDMI 2.0 and HDCP 2.2 (your TV, streaming device, audio/video receiver, cables, adapters, and any other connected devices). Try checking for updates and switching the audio. Here’s how: 1. Make sure your software is up to date by pressing the Home button on your Roku remote, then go to Settings > System > System Update > Check now. 2. Try switching the audio on your Roku or stereo. Press the Home button on your Roku remote, then go to Settings > Audio > Audio Mode > Stereo.For more info, go to Roku’s Surround sound article.

Note For Dolby Atmos audio, all devices must support HDMI 2.0 and HDCP 2.2 (your TV, streaming device, audio/video receiver, cables, adapters, and any other connected devices). HBO Max loads slow, buffers, or pauses

HBO Max loads slow, buffers, or pauses

If videos buffer or are poor quality, a slow or inconsistent internet connection might be the cause. ##### Choose the device you’re using: If HBO Max is buffering, here are some things to try. You may need to try more than one solution to fix the issue: - Android: Open the Play Store on your Android device and search for HBO Max. If an app update is available choose Update. - iOS: Open the App Store on your Apple device and search for HBO Max. If an app update is available choose Update. - Amazon Fire tablet: Open the Amazon Appstore on your Fire tablet and search for HBO Max. If an app update is available, choose Update.

Once updated, try using HBO Max again. - Android: Open Settings, then choose System > Software Update (steps vary based on your Android version). - iOS: Open Settings, then choose General > Software Update. - Amazon Fire tablet: Open Settings, then choose Device Options > System Updates.

Once updated, try using HBO Max again. Here’s how to test your internet connection speed: - Android: Download an Internet speed test app from the Google Play Store and test your connection speed. - iOS: Download an Internet speed test app from the Apple App Store and test your connection speed. - Amazon Fire tablet: From the Home screen on your Fire tablet, swipe down and then select the wireless icon. Tap the network that you’re connected to and check the signal strength and link speed.

To stream HD video, you need a minimum download speed of 5 Mbps. Cellular connection? If you’re using a cellular data connection, try connecting to a wireless network and then try using HBO Max again. ###### Reset your Wi-Fi connection If your phone or tablet is connected to a wireless network, try the following: 1. Turn on Airplane mode on your phone or tablet. 2. Wait 10 seconds, then turn off Airplane mode. 3. Open HBO Max and try watching something.

Switch to a Wi-Fi connection

If you’re using a cellular data connection, try connecting to a wireless network to see if playback is improved. To do this, on your phone or tablet go to Settings and connect to a Wi-Fi network. If other people are using the same Wi-Fi network, ask them to temporarily pause their activity. This will allocate as much bandwidth as possible for streaming HBO Max. Restart your phone or tablet and network devices. Here’s how: 1. Turn off your phone or tablet with HBO Max. 2. Unplug the power from your network devices (modem, router, etc.). 3. Wait 30 seconds. 4. Reconnect the power to your modem and wait for it to connect to the internet (about 1 minute). 5. Reconnect the power to your router and wait for it to connect to the internet. 6. Turn on your phone or tablet, open HBO Max, and try watching something.

Try streaming HBO Max on another supported device connected to the same network. This will help determine if the problem is related to your network or a specific device. If buffering only happens on your phone or tablet, delete the HBO Max app from your phone or tablet and install it again from the app store. Then, open HBO Max and try watching something. If HBOMax.com is buffering, here are some things to try. You may need to try more than one solution to fix the issue: For the best experience, we recommend using a supported browser. If you’re using a supported browser, update your browser to the latest version. To find out how to do this, search the internet for “update” + the name of your browser (e.g., update Chrome). Try using a different supported browser (Chrome, Firefox, Microsoft Edge, or Safari). If video plays successfully with a different browser, check your browser configuration: - Make sure third-party cookies are enabled. - Disable ad-blocking software. - Turn off private browsing or incognito mode. - Disable browser plug-ins, extensions, and add-ons.

Close and reopen your browser, then try watching something. Test your internet connection to make sure you have a minimum download speed of 5 Mbps. To do this, open a browser and search for ‘Internet speed test.’ If your download speed doesn’t meet the minimum, contact your internet provider to troubleshoot your connection speed. If other people are using the same network, ask them to temporarily pause their activity. This will allocate as much bandwidth as possible for streaming HBO Max. Restart your computer and network devices. Here’s how: 1. Close all apps and restart your computer. 2. Unplug the power from your network devices (e.g. modem, router, etc.). 3. Wait 30 seconds. 4. Reconnect the power to your modem and wait for it to connect to the internet (about 1 minute). 5. Reconnect the power to your router and wait for it to connect to the internet. 6. Turn on your computer, go to HBOMax.com, and try watching something.

For the best performance, try using a wired connection with your computer (instead of a Wi-Fi network). To do this, connect your computer to your router using an Ethernet cable. Make sure the cables to your network devices (e.g. modem, router, etc.) are all tightly connected at both connection points. A loose coaxial or Ethernet cable can weaken the signal. In the lower-right corner, choose More Info > Update. - Amazon Fire TV: On your Fire TV, go to Settings > My Fire TV > About > Check for System Update.

Go to Settings > Network > Test network. - Test your download speed from a computer on the same network as your Sharp TiVo TV. To do this, open a browser and search for ‘Internet speed test.’

To stream HD video, you need a minimum download speed of 5 Mbps. For the best 4K streaming experience, we recommend a download speed of 50+ Mbps. If other people are using the same network, ask them to temporarily pause their activity. This will allocate as much bandwidth as possible for streaming HBO Max. Restart your TV and network devices. Here’s how: 1. Unplug your Sharp TiVo TV. 2. Unplug the power from your network devices (modem, router, etc.). 3. Wait 30 seconds. 4. Reconnect the power to your modem and wait for it to connect to the internet (about 1 minute). 5. Reconnect the power to your router and wait for it to connect to the internet. 6. Plug your Sharp TiVo TV device back in, open HBO Max and try watching something.

If your Sharp TiVo TV has an Ethernet port, try connecting your device directly to your router with an Ethernet cable (instead of using Wi-Fi). Make sure the cables to your network devices (modem, router, etc.) are all tightly connected at both connection points. A loose coaxial or Ethernet cable can weaken the signal. Try streaming HBO Max on another supported device connected to the same network. This will help determine if the problem is related to your network or a specific device. If you have a Sony TV with Android TV built-in, try the Android TV steps above. First, make sure your HBO Max app and VIZIO TV are up-to-date. - HBO Max app: The HBO Max app on VIZIO TVs automatically updates when available. To manually check for an update, press the Menu button (Gear icon) on your remote, then choose System > Check for Updates. - VIZIO TV: On your VIZIO TV remote, press the Menu button (Gear icon), then choose System > Check for Updates.

Then, open HBO Max and try watching something. On your remote, press the Menu button (gear icon), then go to Network > Network Test or Test Connection. To stream HD video, you need a minimum download speed of 5 Mbps. For the best 4K streaming experience, we recommend a download speed of 50+ Mbps. If other people are using the same network, ask them to temporarily pause their activity. This will allocate as much bandwidth as possible for streaming HBO Max. Restart your TV and network devices. Here’s how: 1. Unplug your VIZIO TV. 2. Hold the power button on the side of the TV down for 3-5 seconds. 3. Reconnect the power cord and turn on the TV. 4. Open HBO Max, sign in, and try using the app again.

Some VIZIO TVs can also be power cycled from the menu. To do this, press the Menu button (Gear icon) on your remote, then choose System > Reset & Admin > Soft Power Cycle. Try streaming HBO Max on another supported device connected to the same network. This will help determine if the problem is related to your network or a specific device. Missing episode on HBO Max

Missing episode on HBO Max

Select content appears on HBO Max for a limited time. If a series episode isn’t available on HBO Max, try the following: Browse through the episodes on the series page. Here’s how: 1. In HBO Max, choose the Search icon (image: magnifying glass) and search for the series by name (e.g., Game of Thrones). 2. On the Episodes tab, choose a Season number and then browse the episodes in that season.

Most new HBO shows and movies are available on HBO Max when they premiere on HBO. To find out when new episodes will premiere on HBO, go to HBO.com and search for a series by name. New episodes for some HBO shows aren’t available on HBO Max when they air on TV. Instead, they’re added to HBO Max as quickly as possible, typically within 24 hours. Estimated availability times for new episodes: - Hard Knocks: Training Camp with the Buffalo Bills: Tuesdays, 9pm ET - Last Week Tonight with John Oliver: Mondays, 2am ET - Real Time with Bill Maher: Saturdays, 7am ET

Episodes aired within the last year are available on HBO Max. Try updating your HBO Max app. To do this, open the app store on your device and search for HBO Max. If there’s an app update available, download it. For help updating HBO Max, go to Supported devices. Check if the episode is available on another device. To do this, open HBO Max on another device, search for the series name, and browse the available episodes. If an episode is only missing from your Android or Amazon Fire device, try clearing the HBO Max app cache and data. On your Android phone or tablet: 1. Open Settings and search for HBO Max. 2. Choose HBO Max from the search results. 3. Choose Storage & cache or Storage (depending on your Android OS version). 4. Choose Clear cache. 5. Choose Clear storage or Clear data (if available). 6. Open HBO Max, sign in, and browse the series episodes.

On your Amazon Fire tablet: 1. Open Settings > Apps & Games > Manage All Applications. 2. Choose HBO Max from the list of apps. 3. Choose Force Stop. 4. Choose Clear Cache. 5. Choose Clear Data. 6. Open HBO Max, sign in, and browse the series episodes.

Let us know

If you still can’t find the episode you’re looking for, please let us know. Go to our Feedback page, choose Programming feedback as the topic, and let us know what episode is missing. ##### Continue Watching When you finish watching an episode of a series, the next episode is automatically added to Continue Watching. For more info, go to My List and Continue Watching.

Something went wrong message or error code

Something went wrong message or error code

  • Need help signing in?

If you’re getting a ‘Something went wrong’ message or an HBO Max error code, try these solutions: ##### Things to try You may need to try more than one of these solutions to resolve the issue. - Phone, tablet, or TV device: Force close HBO Max, then reopen HBO Max and try streaming again. - Computer: Close and reopen your browser, then go back to HBOMax.com and try streaming again.

Try streaming different shows and movies to find out if the error happens with all titles, or only a particular show or movie. If the error only happens with a particular show or movie, please contact us and let us know. Make sure you have the latest version of HBO Max. Here’s how: - Computer: Try updating your browser. To find out how, go to Troubleshooting HBOMax.com. - Roku: On your Roku, highlight the HBO Max channel, press the Star button (*) on your remote, and then choose Check for updates. Once updated, try using HBO Max again. - Other device: Open the app store on your device, search for HBO Max, and check if there’s an update available. If there’s an app update, download it, and try using HBO Max again.

  1. Do one of the following:Phone, tablet, or computer: Choose your profile and then Sign Out.
  2. TV device: Choose the Settings icon (image: cog (lower left)) and then Sign Out.
  3. Turn your device off.
  4. Wait 30 seconds and turn it back on.
  5. Open HBO Max, sign in, and try streaming again.

Try HBO Max on another supported device. If the error doesn’t happen on another device, go to Troubleshoot HBO Max. Using a computer? Try using a different browser (Chrome, Firefox, Microsoft Edge, or Safari). Choose the device you’re using and try the troubleshooting steps: - Phone or tablet - Computer - TV device

  1. Unplug the power from your network devices (modem, router, etc.).
  2. Wait 30 seconds.
  3. Reconnect the power to your modem and wait for it to connect to the internet (about 1 minute).
  4. After your modem has finished connecting, reconnect the power to your router and wait for it to connect to the internet.
  5. Open HBO Max and try streaming again.

Take note of the on-screen error message or code and include it when you contact us.

Streaming on too many devices message

Streaming on too many devices message

You can stream HBO Max on multiple devices at the same time. If you get the ‘Your account is streaming on too many devices’ message, it means you’ve reached the maximum number of devices that your household can stream at the same time for your plan. The number of devices that your household can stream on at once depends on your subscription plan: - Plan: Number of streams - Basic with Ads: 2 - Standard: 2 - Premium: 4

Watching sports is limited to 2 devices at once Note Extra member accounts are limited to 1 stream and aren’t included in the number of streams the account holder has. For more info, go to Extra Member Add-On. Here’s how to find your subscription plan: 1. In HBO Max, do one of the following:Phone, tablet, or computer: Choose your profile and then Subscription. 2. TV device: Choose the Settings icon (image: cog (lower left)) and then Subscription.(Only Adult profiles can manage devices.) 3. Find your current plan. Explore HBO Max plans.

To start streaming on another device, ask someone in your household to stop streaming HBO Max. (After they stop streaming, you may need to wait a few minutes before you can start streaming on your device.) For a list of devices signed in to your account: 1. Sign in to HBO Max on your phone, tablet, or computer. Can’t sign in? 2. Choose your profile, then Account and Devices.(Only Adult profiles can manage devices.) 3. Review the list of devices signed in to your account including the profile name, sign-in date and country. 4. To sign your account out of a device, choose the X beside a device.

You can share your HBO Max account with the people who live with you (the people in your household). For more info, go to HBO Max account sharing. For more info about managing devices using your account, go to Manage your devices. ##### Unfamiliar device? If you don’t recognize a device, go to Stop unwanted use of your account.

Troubleshoot the HBO Max app or HBOMax.com

Troubleshoot the HBO Max app or HBOMax.com

  • Need help signing in?
  • Videos buffer or pause
  • HBO Max audio issues

If HBO Max freezes or doesn’t work as expected, try the solutions below. ##### Choose the device you’re using: If HBO Max isn’t working as expected, try the following solutions. You may need to try more than one solution to fix the issue. - Android: Open the Play Store on your Android device and search for HBO Max. If an app update is available, choose Update. - iOS: Open the App Store on your Apple device and search for HBO Max. If an app update is available, choose Update. - Amazon Fire tablet: Open the Amazon Appstore on your Fire tablet and search for HBO Max. If an app update is available, choose Update.

  • Android: Open Settings, then choose System > Software Update (steps vary based on your Android version).
  • iOS: Open Settings, then choose General > Software Update.
  • Amazon Fire tablet: Open Settings, then choose Device Options > System Updates.

Once updated, try using HBO Max again. Here’s how to test your internet connection speed: - Android: Download an Internet speed test app from the Google Play Store and test your connection speed. - iOS: Download an Internet speed test app from the Apple App Store and test your connection speed. - Amazon Fire tablet: From the Home screen on your Fire tablet, swipe down and then select the wireless icon. Tap the network that you’re connected to and check the signal strength and link speed.

You need a minimum download speed of 5 Mbps. If videos are buffering, go to HBO Max buffers or pauses. Cellular connection? If you’re using a cellular data connection, try connecting to a wireless network and then try using HBO Max again. 1. Sign out of HBO Max. To do this, choose your profile and then Sign Out. 2. Hold down the power button until you’re prompted to power off your device (turn the device off instead of putting it in sleep mode). 3. Wait 10 seconds and then turn your device back on. 4. Open HBO Max, sign in, and try using the app again.

Try clearing the HBO Max cache: 1. Open Settings and search for HBO Max. 2. Choose HBO Max from the search results. 3. Choose Storage & cache or Storage (depending on your Android OS version). 4. Choose Clear cache. 5. Choose Clear storage or Clear data (if available). 6. Open HBO Max, sign in, and try using the app again.

  1. Go to Settings > Apps & Games > Manage All Applications.
  2. Choose HBO Max from the list of apps.
  3. Choose Force Stop.
  4. Choose Clear Cache.
  5. Choose Clear Data.
  6. Open HBO Max, sign in, and try using the app again.
