Watch HBO Max: Using HBO Max

Accessibility features on HBO Max

Accessibility features on HBO Max

You can use accessibility features to get around HBO Max and stream shows and movies. HBO Max supports the following: - Closed Captions - Sign Language - Audio description - Screen readers - Keyboard navigation - Alexa voice commands - Photosensitivity warnings

Closed captions

Most shows and movies on HBO Max can be watched with closed captions. During playback, you can turn closed captions on or off by choosing the Audio and Captions button in the video player. For more info, go to Subtitles, captions, and audio. ##### Sign Language Some shows and movies on HBO Max offer American Sign Language (ASL) interpretation. To find programming that supports ASL, search for “ASL” in HBO Max. If a show or movie supports ASL, you’ll see (with ASL) along with a badge on the image. ##### Audio Description (Narration) Audio description is an alternate audio track that describes what’s happening on screen in between dialogue (for example, a detailed narration of the scene or action that’s taking place). HBO Max provides more than 8,000 viewing hours of Audio Descriptive Content. Our library is always growing. - In HBO Max, choose the Search icon (image: magnifying glass) and then type DVS or Audio Description, or choose the Audio Description category.

Shows and movies with audio descriptions will have an Audio Description indicator on the details page. ###### Turn Audio description on or off You can turn on Audio Description for select shows and movies. When you turn on Audio Description, it plays as the default audio track for other shows and movies you watch (if available). Here’s how: 1. Start playing a show or movie, then bring up the player controls. 2. Choose the Audio and Subtitles menu (image: speech bubble). 3. In the Audio section, choose [Language] - Audio Description (if available), then select the close subtitles and audio popup button (X) to close.

To turn off audio descriptions, re-open Audio and Subtitles menu and choose any other language in the Audio section. If you’ve turned off audio description in HBO Max, but it keeps playing, check your device settings. For example: - On your phone or tablet, go to Settings > Accessibility and then turn off Audio Description.

Roku: Play a show or movie, then press the Star button (*) on your Roku remote to bring up the Accessibility and Captions options. ###### Finding shows and movies with Audio Description Search: - In HBO Max, choose the search icon (image: magnifying glass) and then type DVS or Audio Description, or choose the Audio Description category.

From the Home screen: - TV devices: On the Home screen, choose Categories (left edge), find the Browse by Genre row, then choose Audio Description. - Phone, tablet, or computer: On the Home screen, open the Browse menu (image: 3 horizontal lines) and choose Series or Movies, then scroll right to find Audio Description (below the large image).

From HBOMax.com: Are you signed in to HBOMax.com? You can find the Audio Description collection at play.hbomax.com/audio-description. You can also find the Audio Description collection this way: 1. On the HBOMax.com Home page, press Tab to bring up the focus. Then, press Tab again to focus on Browse Audio Description. 2. Press Space to select Browse Audio Description. This will take you to the Audio Description collection.You can find the Browse Audio Description button at the top of most pages on HBOMax.com (the menu is hidden until you access it via keyboard navigation). Skip to Main Content and Browse Audio Description aren’t available while you watch programming.Screen readersYou can use screen readers to help you find and play titles. HBO Max supports the following screen readers:Android phone or tablet, Android TV: TalkBack 3. Apple iPad, iPhone, Apple TV: VoiceOverWhile using VoiceOver, you can use Screen Curtain to turn off your display and continue streaming HBO Max. 4. Comcast: Voice Guidance 5. Cox: Voice Guidance 6. DIRECTV: TalkBack 7. FireTV: VoiceView 8. Hisense VIDAA TV: Text-to-Speech 9. PlayStation 5: Screen Reader 10. Roku: Screen Reader 11. Samsung: Voice Guide 12. Web: NVDA, JAWS, VoiceOver, TalkBack 13. Xbox: Narrator 14. Press Tab or the Arrow keys to move between items. 15. Press Spacebar to choose the selected item. 16. Press Enter to activate links. 17. Press Esc to close the menus and overlays. 18. Use the Arrow keys to navigate and the Enter key to select. 19. Keyboard shortcuts are not available at this time. 20. Amazon Fire TV device 21. Alexa enabled device, Alexa voice remote, or a paired Echo device 22. Make sure your Amazon Fire TV is up to date. To do this, go to Settings > Device > About > Check for System Update. 23. Make sure your Alexa device is linked to your Fire TV. Find out how at Use Your Alexa Device to Control Your Fire TV.

For more info, go to Alexa Help. ###### Photosensitivity warnings Some shows and movies with intense flash patterns have a photosensitivity warning on the title details screen and in the video player when you first start watching. The photosensitivity warning in the video player appears in the top corner after the ratings. A portion of our shows and movies have gone through our new process of receiving these warnings. Some devices automatically play previews while you browse HBO Max. The autoplay previews don’t include photosensitivity warnings. When you first encounter an autoplay preview on the web, you can stop it by hitting the Spacebar. On TV devices, you can stop an autoplay preview by navigating left to one of the global navigation items. You can turn off Autoplay Previews in the HBO Max app settings on your device. For instructions, go to HBO Max settings.

Blackout restrictions on HBO Max

Blackout restrictions on HBO Max

Depending on your location, some live sports games can’t be streamed due to league blackout policies. A game may be unavailable in a location regardless of where it’s being played. To find out if you can watch a game from your location, go to our Digital Assistant Game lookup tool. If you enter your ZIP Code, we’ll let you know if you can watch the game from your location. If a live game is unavailable at your location, you’ll get a blackout restriction message when you try to watch it. If a game is blacked out for your location, check your local listings to find out where the event is being broadcast in your area. ##### ‘This event is blacked out in your location’ message If you’re getting a ‘This event is blacked out in your location’ message when you try to watch a game, there may be a blackout restriction for your location. If you don’t think you should be getting a blackout restriction message, choose the device you’re using and follow the steps below. ###### Choose the device you’re using: Try the following steps: First, find out if a game has blackout restrictions for your location. Here’s how: 1. Go to our Digital Assistant Game lookup tool, and enter the team or league you’re trying to watch (e.g. enter Denver Avalanche or NHL). 2. Pick the game you want to watch and we’ll check for blackout restrictions. 3. If the game has blackout restrictions, enter your ZIP Code.

If there isn’t a blackout restriction for your ZIP Code, go to Step 2. HBO Max determines your location using location-based services. To watch live sports, you need to share your location. Here’s how: - Choose a live game and share your location when prompted.

If you don’t get a prompt to share your location, check your device settings: - Android: Go to Settings > Location > App location permissions and then choose HBO Max. Here you can give HBO Max access to your device’s location. For more info, go to Google’s Location settings article. - iPhone or iPad: Go to Settings > Privacy & Security > Location Services and then choose HBO Max. Here you can give HBO Max access to your device’s location. For more info, go to Apple’s Location services article.

Once your device’s location is shared, try watching the live game again. If a Wi-Fi network is available, try connecting to a wireless network instead of using your cellular data connection (on your device go to Settings and connect to a Wi-Fi network). Once your device is connected to a Wi-Fi network, try watching the live game again. Try restarting your modem and router. Here’s how: 1. Turn off or unplug your phone or tablet with HBO Max. 2. Unplug the power from your network devices (modem, router, etc.). 3. Wait 30 seconds. 4. Reconnect the power to your modem and wait for it to connect to the internet (about a minute). 5. Reconnect the power to your router and wait for it to connect to the internet. 6. Turn your phone or tablet back on, open HBO Max and try watching the live game again.

If you still get a blackout restriction message, gather the following info and contact us via our Digital Assistant. - Your Internet Service Provider (ISP) - Your IP address (open a browser and type ‘what is my IP address’ to get this)

Try the following steps: First, find out if the game has blackout restrictions for your location. Here’s how: 1. Go to our Digital Assistant Game lookup tool, and enter the team or league you’re trying to watch (e.g. enter Denver Avalanche or NHL). 2. Pick the game you want to watch and we’ll check for blackout restrictions. 3. If the game has blackout restrictions, enter your ZIP Code.

If there isn’t a blackout restriction for your ZIP Code, go to Step 2. If the game doesn’t have blackout restrictions for your location, your IP address might be blocked or incorrectly identified. To fix this, disable your VPN, proxy, or anonymization service so that your IP address is not blocked. Now, try watching the live game again. Try restarting your modem and router. Here’s how: 1. Turn off or unplug your computer. 2. Unplug the power from your network devices (modem, router, etc.). 3. Wait 30 seconds. 4. Reconnect the power to your modem and wait for it to connect to the internet (about a minute). 5. Reconnect the power to your router and wait for it to connect to the internet. 6. Turn your computer back on, go to HBOMax.com and try watching the live game again.

If you still get a blackout restriction message, gather the following info and contact us via our Digital Assistant. - Your Internet Service Provider (ISP) - Your IP address (open a browser and type ‘what is my IP address’ to get this)

Try the following steps: First, find out if the game has blackout restrictions for your location. Here’s how: 1. On your phone, tablet, or computer, go to our Digital Assistant Game lookup tool, and enter the team or league you’re trying to watch (e.g. enter Denver Avalanche or NHL). 2. Pick the game you want to watch and we’ll check for blackout restrictions. 3. If the game has blackout restrictions, enter your ZIP Code.

If there isn’t a blackout restriction for your ZIP Code, go to Step 2. If the game doesn’t have blackout restrictions for your location, your IP address might be blocked or incorrectly identified. To fix this, disable your VPN, proxy, or anonymization service so that your IP address is not blocked. Now, try watching the live game again. Try restarting your modem and router. Here’s how: 1. Turn off or unplug your TV. 2. Unplug the power from your network devices (modem, router, etc.). 3. Wait 30 seconds. 4. Reconnect the power to your modem and wait for it to connect to the internet (about a minute). 5. Reconnect the power to your router and wait for it to connect to the internet. 6. Turn your TV back on, open the HBO Max app and try watching the live game again.

If you still get a blackout restriction message, gather the following info and contact us via our Digital Assistant. - Your Internet Service Provider (ISP) - Your IP address (open a browser and type ‘what is my IP address’ to get this)

Content ratings on HBO Max

Content ratings on HBO Max

Content ratings can help you make informed streaming decisions for you and your family. - Ratings are set by HBO Max or by local content rating organizations. - HBO Max has a dedicated team that reviews content and works with local organizations to set the most appropriate rating for each country. - HBO Max includes content descriptors, when appropriate and necessary. - Ratings may vary by country.

Where can I find ratings in HBO Max?

Content ratings appear throughout HBO Max. Here’s some of the places you’ll find ratings: - On the title’s description and more info screen - In the upper-left corner when you first start watching something - In search results - In the video player - On the Home screen

TV and movie ratings

In the United States, HBO Max uses the TV Parental Guideline ratings and the Motion Picture Association ratings. - Y (All Children): This program is designed to be appropriate for all children. - Y7 (Older Children): This program is designed for children aged 7 and above. - Y7-FV (Older Children: Fantasy Violence): This program is designed for older children (age 7+) and contains fantasy violence. - G (General Audience): Most parents would find this program appropriate for all ages. - PG (Parental Guidance): This program contains material that parents may find unsuitable for younger children. - 14 (Parents Strongly Cautioned): This program contains some material that parents would find unsuitable for children under 14 years of age. - MA (Mature Audience Only): This show is specifically designed to be viewed by adults and therefore may be unsuitable for children under 17.

For more info about these TV ratings, go to thetvboss.org/tv-ratings. TV series ratings: The ratings on a TV series details screen are the highest maturity rating for an episode in the series. - G (General Audiences): Nothing that would offend parents for viewing by children. - PG (Parental Guidance Suggested): Parents urged to give “parental guidance.” May contain some material parents might not like for their young children. - PG-13 (Parents Strongly Cautioned): Parents are urged to be cautious. Some material may be inappropriate for pre-teenagers. - R (Restricted): Contains some adult material. Parents are urged to learn more about the film before taking their young children with them. - NC-17: Clearly adult. Children are not admitted.

For more info about the Motion Picture Association ratings, go to motionpictures.org/film-ratings. ##### Ratings and traveling Content ratings are based on your physical location. If you travel to a country with a different content rating system, shows and movies may be rated differently. ##### Set ratings for a Kids profile You can create a Kids profile that limits what content is available to your child and what content they can watch. When you edit a Kids profile, you can choose from the following rating groups: - G, TV-Y (Up to Age 5): Content is designed for the youngest viewers. - G, TV-Y7, TV-G (Ages 6+): Content may include mild fantasy or comedic violence. Contains little or no strong language or sexual content. - PG, TV-PG (Ages 10+): Content may contain mild language, suggestive dialogue, sexual situations, and/or moderate violence. - PG-13, TV-14 (Ages 13+): Content may contain coarse language, suggestive dialogue, sexual situations, and/or intense violence.

By default, a new Kids profile is set to PG and TV-PG (Ages 10+) ratings. When you set ratings for a profile, that profile can only find and watch episodes and movies at or below the ratings you selected. Ratings can also be set for an Adult profile. By default, a new Adult profile can watch all content ratings. To change ratings for a profile, you need to Edit the profile. ##### Content ratings not working? If the ratings you set for a Kids profile aren’t working as expected, check the following: - Check to see if restrictions are set on your device (e.g., on iOS check for Parental controls). If the device your child is using has parental controls that are more restrictive than the Ratings you set in HBO Max, HBO Max will follow the parental controls set on the device. - HBO Max doesn’t follow parental controls that you may have set with your TV provider.

Download HBO Max shows and movies

Download HBO Max shows and movies

You can download HBO Max shows and movies on the following mobile devices and watch them when you’re offline: - Apple iPhone, iPad, and iPod Touch - Android phone and tablet - Amazon Fire tablet

Need help downloading the HBO Max app? Go to Install HBO Max on supported devices. ##### Downloads Q&A Here’s the number of downloads your account can have at one time (across all mobile devices) for each subscription plan. - HBO Max Plan: Number of Downloads - Basic with Ads: 0 - Standard: 30 - Premium: 100

Certain series may be limited to 25 downloaded episodes from the same series. The Standard and Premium plans have a limit of 30 downloaded movies (at one time across all mobile devices). Pay-per-view replays are not available for download. What you need: - Standard or Premium plan - An internet connection (a strong Wi-Fi connection is best) - Phone or tablet with enough storage space for the title(s) you want to download

Here’s how to download a show or movie: 1. Sign in to HBO Max on your phone or tablet. Can’t sign in? 2. Find something to download. Titles that can be downloaded have a Download icon (image: arrow pointing down) beside the movie or episode description. Can’t find the Download icon? 3. To check which languages are available for a title, choose the Play icon and then choose the Audio and Subtitles icon (image: speech bubble) in the video player. Select your preferred language for Subtitles and Audio, then go back to the content description screen. 4. Choose the Download icon (image: arrow pointing down) for the title. The Download icon updates to show you the download progress. To cancel a download, choose the Download progress icon (image: animated circle) and then Cancel.

Storage
  • Downloads are stored on your phone or tablet’s internal storage (downloads can’t be saved to an SD card).
  • To find your available storage space: Open HBO Max, choose your profile, then App Settings > Download Settings.
  • The amount of storage space you need depends on the length of the title and the download video quality.

Need help? Go to Troubleshooting. HBO Max downloads include the original audio track, subtitles and audio in the language you last selected in HBO Max (if available), and English closed captions. For example, if you start watching an episode with Spanish audio and subtitles and then download the episode, HBO Max will download Spanish audio and subtitles (as well as English if it’s the original audio track). 1. In HBO Max, choose the Downloads icon (image: arrow pointing down (bottom edge)).If the current profile is an Adult profile, you’ll find downloads for each profile on your device. Kids profiles can only watch shows and movies downloaded by their profile. 2. Choose the download that you want to watch.

Once you start watching a download, you have 48 hours to finish watching it before it expires. Unwatched downloads expire 30 days after they’re downloaded. Once expired, you can renew or delete the download. Need help? Go to Can’t play a download. When a download expires, you’ll need to renew it before you can watch it. Here’s how: 1. In HBO Max, choose the Downloads icon (image: arrow pointing down (bottom edge)). 2. Choose the Expired download icon (image: clock) on the download you want to renew. 3. Choose Renew Download (an internet connection is needed to renew a download).

Things to know - Unwatched downloads expire 30 days after downloading (or when a show or movie leaves HBO Max). - Once you start watching a download, you have 48 hours to finish watching it before it expires. - You can renew a download multiple times.

Need help? Go to Can’t renew a download. 1. In HBO Max, choose the Downloads icon (image: arrow pointing down (bottom edge)). 2. Then, do one of the following:Choose the Download complete icon (image: check mark) beside a download and then choose Delete Download. 3. Choose the series name, the Edit button, and then choose the X beside the episode(s) you want to delete.

If you no longer have access to a device, you can sign out of a device (however this doesn’t delete the downloads on the device). Unwatched downloads automatically expire after 30 days. ##### Basic with Ads plan If you change your subscription plan to Basic with Ads, your downloads will be deleted. You can watch shows and movies that you’ve already downloaded in any country or region. Here’s how: 1. Before you travel, download shows and movies on your phone or tablet. 2. While traveling, open HBO Max (sign in if needed), and choose the Downloads icon (image: arrow pointing down (bottom edge)). 3. Choose the download you want to watch.

You can stream and download shows and movies in countries and regions where HBO Max is available. For more info, go to Where is HBO Max available? ##### Download settings To change the video quality of downloads: 1. In HBO Max, choose your profile. 2. Choose App Settings > Download Settings > Video Quality. 3. Choose a download quality: Good, Better, or Best (the default setting is Good).

This setting applies to all profiles on your phone or tablet. Because downloads use a large amount of data, you may want to restrict downloads to Wi-Fi (instead of using your cellular network). To change the Download Over Wi-Fi Only setting: 1. In HBO Max, choose your profile. 2. Choose App Settings > Download Settings. 3. Turn Download Over Wi-Fi Only on or off.

This setting applies to all profiles on your phone or tablet. ##### Troubleshooting If you can’t find the Download icon (image: arrow pointing down) for an episode or movie, there could be a few different reasons: - The device you’re using doesn’t support downloads (you can’t download shows or movies on a computer or TV device). Downloads are only available on:Apple iPhone, iPad, and iPod Touch - Most Android phones and tablets - Amazon Fire tablets

You have the Basic with Ads plan. Which plan do I have? The episode or movie isn’t available for download. Select episodes and movies aren’t available to download. Pay-per-view events can’t be downloaded. First, make sure the download hasn’t expired (if expired, connect to the internet and renew the download). Next, try the following steps on your phone or tablet: 1. Make sure you’re signed in. 2. Close the HBO Max app. 3. Turn on Airplane mode on your phone or tablet. 4. Turn off Wi-Fi. 5. Open HBO Max and try playing a download.

If this doesn’t help and you’re using an Android phone or tablet, try clearing the HBO Max cache. Here’s how: 1. On your Android phone or tablet, open Settings and search for HBO Max. 2. Choose HBO Max from the search results. 3. Choose Storage & cache or Storage (depending on your Android OS version). 4. Choose Clear cache. 5. Open HBO Max, sign in, and try playing a download.

If you’re using a cellular data connection, connect to a wireless network and start the download over again. If this doesn’t help, go to Troubleshoot HBO Max and try the troubleshooting suggestions. This message means your account has reached the maximum number of downloads for your subscription plan across all phones and tablets. To fix this, do the following: 1. First, find out which subscription plan you have. Which plan do I have?If you have the Standard plan, your account can have 30 downloads at one time across all phones and tablets signed in to your account. If you have the Premium plan, your account can have 100 downloads at one time across all phones and tablets signed in to your account. 2. Check the number of downloads on all mobile devices signed in to your account. For a list of phones and tablets signed in to your account, go to Manage your devices.

‘Episode Limit Reached’ message Some series have a limit of 25 downloaded episodes (at one time across all mobile devices). If your current device doesn’t have 25 downloaded episodes from the same series, check the downloads on other phones and tablets signed in to your account. For a list of devices signed in to your account, go to Manage your devices. ‘Movie Limit Reached’ message The Standard and Premium plans have a limit of 30 downloaded movies (at one time across all mobile devices). If your current device doesn’t have 30 downloaded movies, check the downloads on other phones and tablets signed in to your account. If you get this message, check your available storage space and try downloading a single episode. To find your available storage space: - Open HBO Max on your phone or tablet, choose your profile, then App Settings > Download Settings. For more detailed info, open Settings on your iOS or Android phone or tablet and search for Storage. - The amount of storage space needed to download a title depends on the length and the video quality. For example, an hour-long download with Good video quality can take 1-2 GB of storage.

Storage

Downloads are stored on your phone or tablet’s internal storage (downloads can’t be saved to an SD card). If this information doesn’t help, please contact us for help. To renew a download, you need to be: - Located in a country or region where the title is available - Connected to the internet - Signed in to HBO Max - Located in a country where HBO Max is available

In HBO Max, use search to find out if a title is available. If a title is no longer available in the country/region where you’re located, you can’t renew the download.

HBO Max on Apple Vision Pro

HBO Max on Apple Vision Pro

HBO Max is supported on the Apple Vision Pro. You can download the HBO Max app, sign in, and start streaming HBO Max. ##### Install and update Install: On your Apple Vision Pro, open the App Store and search for HBO Max. Choose HBO Max and then the download option. Update: On your Apple Vision Pro, open the App store and search for HBO Max. If an app update is available, choose Update. ##### App navigation and playback You can navigate the HBO Max app using Apple Vision Pro gesture controls. - Use your fingers to interact with the HBO Max interface. - Pinch your index finger and thumb then pull up or down to scroll.

Search: Open HBO Max, choose the Search icon (image: magnifying glass), and then use the virtual keyboard or Siri. Playback: You can use gestures, choose the player controls in the HBO Max app, or use Siri (e.g. “Hey Siri, fast-forward 5 minutes”). Volume: Look at the volume indicator in the app and then use the crown to adjust the volume up or down. You can also ask Siri to change the volume (e.g. “Hey Siri, turn the volume down 10%”). ##### Subtitles, captions, and audio To change subtitles, closed captions, or audio: 1. Start watching something and then choose the Audio and Subtitles icon (image: speech bubble). 2. Do one of the following:Subtitles: Under Subtitles, choose a language or Off. 3. Closed captions: Under Subtitles, choose a caption language (e.g., English - CC) or Off. 4. Audio: Under Audio, choose a language (to turn off narration, choose a language that doesn’t include Audio Description). 5. Choose X to save your changes.

Note You can ask Siri to turn subtitles on or off. Say “Hey Siri, turn subtitles on,” or “Hey Siri, turn subtitles off.”

My List and Continue Watching

My List and Continue Watching

Each HBO Max profile has a My Stuff area with My List and Continue Watching. - My List: A list you create of things you want to watch. - Continue Watching: An automatic list of things that you haven’t finished watching.

My List

When you find something you want to watch, you can add it to My List. Later, you can quickly find something you want to watch. Do either of the following: - In HBO Max, choose the My Stuff icon (image: badge). - On the Home screen, find the My List row.

Scroll through the titles you’ve added and choose something to watch. Titles are arranged in the order that you added them (the most recent title is first). ###### Things to know - Each profile has its own My List. If titles are missing, check which profile is active. - Titles remain on My List until you remove them or they’re no longer available on HBO Max (use Search(image: magnifying glass) to find out if a title is available on HBO Max).

  1. In HBO Max, find a title you want to add.
  2. Do one of the following:Phone, tablet, or computer: Choose the Menu icon (image: three vertical dots) on the title and then choose + Add to My List.
  3. TV device: Press and hold the title you want to add.

You can also choose the Add to My List icon (image: plus symbol) on the title details screen. If you want to watch more than a few episodes of a series, consider adding the series instead of individual episodes. 1. In HBO Max, choose the My Stuff icon (image: badge) or on the Home screen find the My List row. 2. Do one of the following:Phone, tablet, or computer: Choose the Menu icon (image: three vertical dots) on the item you want to remove and then choose Remove. 3. Roku: Press the ***** button on your remote. 4. Other TV device: Press and hold the title you want to remove for 1 second.

You can also choose the Check mark icon (image: check mark) (phone or tablet) or Remove from My List on the title details screen. ##### Continue Watching Continue Watching automatically includes shows and movies you didn’t finish watching, as well as the next episode in a series that you’re watching. This way you can easily return to where you stopped watching. Note Titles are automatically removed from Continue Watching after 90 days. In HBO Max, do either of the following: - Choose the My Stuff icon (image: badge) and then Continue Watching. - On the Home screen, find the Continue Watching row.

Scroll through the titles and choose something to watch. 1. In HBO Max, choose the My Stuff icon (image: badge) or on the Home screen find the Continue Watching row. 2. Do one of the following:Phone, tablet, or computer: Choose the Menu icon (image: three vertical dots) on the item you want to remove and then choose Remove. 3. Roku: Press the ***** button on your remote. 4. Other TV device: Press and hold the title you want to remove for 1 second.

Playback options and controls

Playback options and controls

HBO Max includes a number of playback options. ##### Which device are you using? When you’re watching something, tap the screen to access the following: Choose the Play/Pause button to toggle between play and pause. All titles play in landscape mode. Choose the Skip forward icon (image: arrow looping forward) or the Skip backward icon (image: arrow looping backwards) to skip forward or backwards by 10 seconds. The progress bar shows you where you are in what you’re watching. You can tap anywhere on the bar, or tap and drag your location (the dot). The time remaining appears in the lower-right corner. Ad breaks: If you have the Basic with Ads plan, ad breaks are marked on the progress bar. For more info, go to Ads on HBO Max. To adjust the volume, use the volume buttons on your phone or tablet. You can use Picture in Picture to watch HBO Max while you use other apps on your phone or tablet. Here’s how: 1. When you’re watching something, swipe up from the bottom of the screen. 2. Start another app. When you do this, the HBO Max window will stay on top.

If Picture-in-Picture isn’t working, you may need to turn it on. Here’s how: - Android: On your Android device, go to Settings and search for Picture. Then choose Picture-in-picture > HBO Max > Allow picture-in-picture. - iOS: On your iOS device, go to Settings > General > Picture in Picture > Start PiP Automatically.

iOS: You can use the Pinch-to-Zoom gesture to make what you’re watching fill the screen on your phone or tablet. To do this, move 2 fingers apart to zoom in and together to zoom out. Watching while zoomed in may cause the video to appear cropped. Options at the top right of the screen: Choose the Alert icon (upper right) to turn alerts on or off. Alerts let you know about key moments in related sports events—for example, when a goal is scored. Choose the alert to start watching the other event. Choose the Google Cast icon (image: rectangle with Wi-Fi symbol) to cast HBO Max from your phone or tablet to your TV. For more info, go to Chromecast HBO Max to your TV. Choose the AirPlay icon (image: (rectangle and triangle)) to stream HBO Max from your iOS device to your AirPlay-2 compatible device. For more info, go to AirPlay HBO Max to your TV. Choose the Audio and Subtitles icon (image: speech bubble) to change the subtitles, captions, or audio track. For more info, go to Subtitles, captions, and audio. When you’re watching something, hover your cursor over the screen to access the following: Click the Play/Pause button to toggle between play and pause. You can also press the Spacebar. Choose the Skip forward icon (image: arrow looping forward) or the Skip backward icon (image: arrow looping backwards) to skip forward or backwards by 10 seconds. You can also press the left or right arrow key. The progress bar shows you where you are in what you’re watching. You can click anywhere on the bar, or click and drag your location (the dot). The remaining time appears in the lower-right corner. Ad breaks: If you have the Basic with Ads plan, ad breaks are marked on the progress bar. For more info, go to Ads on HBO Max. To adjust the volume, hover your mouse over the Sound icon (image: sound speaker) and then click and drag the sound level (the dot). You can also press the up and down arrow key. To mute the volume, click the Sound icon (image: sound speaker) or press M. To unmute, click the sound icon again or press M (the volume returns to the previous level). You can also use the volume controls on your computer. Click the Audio and Subtitles icon (image: speech bubble) to change the subtitles, captions, or audio track. For more info, go to Subtitles, captions, and audio. Click the Full-screen icon (image: box (bottom right)) to expand what you’re streaming. To exit full screen, click the icon again or press the Esc key. Options at the top right of the screen: Choose the Alert icon (upper right) to turn alerts on or off. Alerts let you know about key moments in related sports events—for example, when a goal is scored. Choose the alert to start watching the other event. Choose the Google Cast icon (image: rectangle with Wi-Fi symbol) to cast HBO Max from your computer to your TV. For more info, go to Chromecast HBO Max to your TV. Choose the AirPlay icon (image: (rectangle and triangle)) to stream HBO Max from your Mac to your AirPlay-2 compatible device. For more info, go to AirPlay HBO Max to your TV. Here are the TV playback options: - TV device: Press the D-pad center button or Play/Pause button on your remote. - PlayStation: Press the X button on your PS controller. - Xbox: Press the A button on your Xbox controller.

  • TV device: Press and hold the D-pad right button or fast-forward button to fast forward. Press and hold the D-pad left button or rewind button to rewind.
  • Apple TV: Press and hold the left or right side of the touch surface to fast forward or rewind.
  • PlayStation: Press the R2 button to fast forward and the L2 button to rewind.
  • Roku: Press the forward or rewind button on your remote.

To increase or decrease the forward or rewind speed, press the same button again (up to 4 times). Press the Play button to resume playback. The progress bar shows you where you are in what you’re watching. The remaining time appears in the lower-right corner. 1. Pause your show or movie. 2. Use the left/right navigation on your remote to move forward/backwards on the progress bar.

Ad breaks: If you have the Basic with Ads plan, ad breaks are marked on the progress bar. For more info, go to Ads on HBO Max. Choose the Audio and Subtitles icon (image: speech bubble) to change the subtitles, captions, or audio track. For more info, go to Subtitles, captions, and audio. Choose the Alert icon (upper right) to turn alerts on or off. Alerts let you know about key moments in related sports events—for example, when a goal is scored. Choose the alert to start watching the other event. Other playback options: When you’re done watching an episode, the next episode will automatically play. You can choose Next Episode to start watching right away, or wait 15 seconds and the episode will automatically play. If you want to watch the end credits, dismiss the Next Episode prompt. Here’s how: - Phone, tablet, or computer: Choose the X beside the Next Episode button. - Apple TV: Press the Menu button on your remote. - Other TV device: Press the back button on your remote.

To find out how to turn off autoplay (next episode or previews), go to HBO Max settings. When you start watching something, you can skip the content that plays before your show or movie. - Skip Intro: Skips ahead to the last second of the introduction. - Skip Recap: Skips ahead to the last second of the episode recap.

The Skip button disappears in 10 seconds. To bring back the button, tap or click the screen on your device. ##### Skipping ads You can’t fast forward or skip ads that are part of the Basic with Ads subscription plan. For more info, go to HBO Max plans. When watching a live sports event, you can choose Go Live (lower right) to jump to what’s happening live. Go Live is only available when you pause, rewind, or start a live event from the beginning. Video quality: HBO Max automatically adjusts the video quality based on your network bandwidth and connection speed. For help improving your internet connection, go to HBO Max buffers or pauses. Most shows and movies are available in high definition (HD). Manual video quality settings aren’t currently available. Aspect ratio: Some HBO Max titles may have black bars or letterboxing along the edges of the video. Black bars are used when the aspect ratio that the title was filmed in doesn’t match the aspect ratio of your screen. Black bars are added instead of stretching or clipping the video. - Black bars above and below: This means the title was filmed with an aspect ratio that’s wider than your screen. - Black bars on the sides: This can happen if the title was filmed with a squarish aspect ratio (e.g. 4:3), and your screen is widescreen (16:9) or ultrawide (21:9).

You can’t manually adjust the aspect ratio in HBO Max to remove the black bars. Playback speed: The video playback speed can’t be adjusted. Multiview: Some live sports have multiple live streams on the same screen at once. For help with Multiview, go to Sports on HBO Max. Apple Vision Pro: Go to HBO Max on Apple Vision Pro

Subtitles, captions, and audio

Subtitles, captions, and audio

Some HBO Max shows and movies have subtitles, closed captions, and multiple audio tracks. You can also change the appearance of captions and subtitles. HBO Max plays shows and movies in the last audio language you selected. If you haven’t chosen a language, HBO Max defaults to your selected app language. If your preferences aren’t available, HBO Max plays the English audio track. ##### Audio description (Narration) Audio description narrates what’s happening on screen and is currently available in English only. To turn this off, choose the Audio and Subtitles icon (image: audio-and-subtitles.svg), then choose a language that doesn’t include Audio Description. ##### Which device are you using? To change subtitles, closed captions, or audio: 1. Start watching something and then tap the screen. 2. Choose the Audio and Subtitles icon (image: speech bubble). 3. Do one of the following:Subtitles: Under Subtitles, choose a language or Off. 4. Closed captions: Under Subtitles, choose a caption language (e.g., English - CC) or Off. 5. Audio: Under Audio, choose a language (to turn off narration, choose a language instead of [Language] - Audio Description). 6. Choose X to save your changes.

You can change the caption styling (size, color, and opacity) on your phone or tablet’s device settings. Here’s how: Swipe down from the top of the screen and choose Subtitling Preferences. Open the Settings app, search for Caption, and then choose Caption style. These steps may vary depending on your Android OS version. For more info, go to Android caption preferences. Open the Settings app, then choose Accessibility > Subtitles & Captioning > Style. For more info, go to Subtitles and captioning on iPhone. Note Changes made to caption styling on your phone or tablet don’t carry over to other devices. ##### Subtitles won’t turn off HBO Max follows the subtitle and caption settings set on your device. If you’ve turned off subtitles or captions in HBO Max but they keep appearing, check your device settings. ###### App language and audio descriptions - To change the language of text in the HBO Max app, go to Change the app language. - For info about audio descriptions, go to Accessibility features.

Audio: Under Audio, choose a language (to turn off narration, choose a language instead of [Language] - Audio Description). 6. Choose X to save your changes.

Use SharePlay to watch HBO Max together

Use SharePlay to watch HBO Max together

With SharePlay, you can watch HBO Max together with your friends and family on Apple devices. Playback is synced with everyone you invite to the SharePlay session. You can watch any show, movie, or event that’s available for all participants. ##### What you need To watch together using SharePlay, everyone needs: - Apple device (iPhone, iPad, Apple TV, or Apple Vision Pro) - Standard or Premium plan - HBO Max app with an Adult profile

iPhone and iPad: Watch together

Here’s how to start watching with friends: 1. Open the HBO Max app on your iPhone and find something to watch. 2. On the content details page, tap the Share icon (image: box with arrow pointing up) and then SharePlay. 3. Enter the contacts you want to share with, then choose Messages or FaceTime.If you choose Messages, contacts will get a message asking them to join SharePlay. If you choose FaceTime, contacts will get a FaceTime call. 4. Choose Watch Now to start the SharePlay session.

Join and watch using SharePlay

When you’re asked to join SharePlay: - FaceTime call: Choose Open and then Join SharePlay. - Messages conversation: Tap the show or movie title at the top of the Messages conversation and then choose Open.

The HBO Max app opens and starts playing in sync with everyone. To switch to your Apple TV, choose the AirPlay icon (image: rectangle and triangle) in HBO Max. ##### Things to know - Playback controls such as Pause and Rewind are shared by everyone. - Volume, subtitles, and captions are unique to each viewer.

Watch together on Apple TV or Apple Vision Pro

You can also watch HBO Max together with friends in a FaceTime call on your Apple TV or Apple Vision Pro. For more info: - Watch together on Apple TV - Use SharePlay in FaceTime calls on Apple Vision Pro

Can I watch with people in different countries?

Yes, you can start or join SharePlay with subscribers in any country or region where HBO Max is available. The selection of titles varies based on your physical location. If a title isn’t available for one or more SharePlay participants, you’ll get a ‘Content Not Available’ message. If you get this message, choose a title that’s available for all participants.

Watch HBO Max in 4K UHD & HDR10 or Dolby Vision

Watch HBO Max in 4K UHD & HDR10 or Dolby Vision

You can watch select titles in 4K UHD with HDR10 or Dolby Vision on select devices with the Premium plan. To find out if a title is available in 4K UHD with HDR10 or Dolby Vision, choose the show or movie in HBO Max and then look for 4K UHD, HDR10, or Dolby Vision on the details screen. Note 4K UHD, HDR, and Dolby Atmos availability depends on your plan and device. HBO Max automatically plays the highest quality video available for your device and subscription plan. Titles available in 4K UHD are also available in HD on all supported devices. For info about Dolby Atmos, go to HBO Max audio quality. ##### What you need To stream in 4K UHD and HDR10 or Dolby Vision: - 4K Ultra HD TV that displays Dolby Vision or HDR10 - Supported 4K device - Premium plan - High speed internet (25 Mbps or higher, 50+ Mbps is recommended)

All devices must support HDMI 2.0 and HDCP 2.2 (your TV, media player, audio/video receiver, cables, adapters, and any other connected devices). If you have the above requirements and the title is available in 4K UHD, HBO Max should automatically play the title in 4K UHD. Check your Amazon Fire TV display settings. Here’s how:

  1. Go to Settings > Display & Sounds > Display.
  2. Open the Video Resolution section.
  3. Set your video resolution to Auto. A notification appears if HDCP 2.2 is not detected for your Fire TV’s HDMI connection.

For more info, go to Watch Video in 4K on Your Fire TV. Check your Apple TV video settings. Here’s how: 1. On your Apple TV, go to Settings > Video and Audio > Format. 2. Select 4K HDR (if available). 3. Go to Match Content. Turn on Match Dynamic Range and Match Frame Rate. 4. Choose Check HDMI Connection to confirm support for the selected format.

If your Apple TV doesn’t detect support for 4K HDR, go to Apple’s 4K, HDR, and Dolby Vision on your Apple TV 4K article. Check your display settings. Here’s how: 1. In the upper-right corner of your TV, go to Profile > Settings. 2. Choose Display & Sound. 3. Select Match Content. This should automatically set your device to display content in the native format. You can also manually set the resolution by going to Advanced display settings.

If you don’t have the option to display in 4K HDR, go to Stream 4K Ultra HD content for more info. Check your Cox video settings. Here’s how: 1. Press the Contour button on your Cox remote. 2. Select Settings (image: settings), then go to Device Settings > Video Display > Video Output Resolution. 3. Select 16:9, 2160p60 4k UHD (Best Available).

For help with your LG TV, go to Best picture settings for your LG 4K or 4K OLED - TV. For help with your PlayStation 5, go to PlayStation’s How to change PS5 resolution article. Check your Roku display settings. Here’s how: 1. Press the Home button on your Roku remote. 2. Go to Settings > Display type and choose Auto Detect. 3. If your Roku detects 4K support for your display, choose OK.

If your Roku doesn’t detect your display type, you can try Force output. For more info about setting your display type, go to How do I change the display type on my Roku? For help with your Samsung TV, go to Samsung’s Adjust picture settings article. You can check the resolution of your stream on some VIZIO TV models. Here’s how: 1. Start watching a 4K movie for 1 minute. 2. Press the Info button on your remote. 3. At the top of your screen, you’ll find the resolution streaming to your TV.

If the resolution is less than 2160p (4K), test your network connection. Here’s how: 1. Press the Menu button (Gear icon) on your remote. 2. Go to Network > Test Connection.

To stream 4K video, you need a minimum download speed of 25 Mbps on your VIZIO Smart TV (we recommend a download speed of 50+ Mbps for the best experience). For more help with your VIZIO TV, go to VIZIO’s Image Quality article. Check your X1 or Flex video settings. Here’s how: 1. Press the Xfinity button on your remote. 2. Select Settings (Gear icon), then go to Device Settings > Video Display > Video Output Resolution. 3. Select 16:9, 2160p60 4k UHD (Best Available).

For more info, go to 4K UHD and HDR on Xfinity TV. Make sure your internet download speed is at least 25 Mbps. HBO Max automatically adjusts the video quality based on the download speed of your connection. If your download speed is less than 25 Mbps, the show or movie won’t stream in 4K. Higher speeds will provide more consistent 4K streaming. For the best experience, we recommend a download speed of 50+ Mbps on your streaming device. To find out how to test your connection speed, go to the HBO Max buffering page. Try a wired connection: If you experience slow or inconsistent speeds over Wi-Fi, connect your streaming device to your router using an Ethernet cable. Reduce activity on your network: If other people are using the same network, ask them to temporarily pause their activity. This will allocate as much bandwidth as possible for streaming. If you’re using a streaming player (e.g. Apple TV): - Make sure you’re using a High-Speed 4K HDMI cable. - Make sure your HDMI cable is plugged into a HDCP 2.2 compatible HDMI port on your TV or audio/video receiver. HDCP 2.2 may not be supported by all of the HDMI ports on your TV (refer to your owner’s manual to find out which HDMI port is HDCP 2.2 compatible). - Bypass your audio video receiver, HDMI switch, or sound bar, by connecting your streaming device directly to your TV.

If you’re getting a ‘There’s a problem with your device setup’ message, go to Device setup error. ###### Screen too dark? If live sports or movies in HDR or Dolby Vision are too dark, do one of the following on your TV or streaming player: - Turn up the screen brightness. - Turn off HDR.

If you need help finding the steps for your TV device, search the internet for “adjust brightness” + your device (e.g., adjust brightness Samsung TV), or “turn off HDR” + your device (e.g., turn off HDR Apple TV).

Watch HBO Max: What’s on HBO Max

2026 March Madness® on HBO Max

2026 March Madness® on HBO Max

HBO Max will stream a selection of 2026 March Madness® games from the First Four, First Round, Second Round, Sweet 16, Elite 8, and Final Four (including the championship) games (the live games and studio coverage from TBS, TNT, and truTV). March Madness® coverage will be available to HBO Max subscribers with the Standard or Premium plan. The first live games will stream on Tuesday, March 17. To watch March Madness® games on HBO Max, you need: - Standard or Premium plan - Supported device

Note The Basic with Ads plan does not include March Madness® games. To find out how to change your plan, go to HBO Max plans. If you don’t have an HBO Max subscription, go to How do I subscribe to HBO Max? ##### How do I watch March Madness® on HBO Max? Standard and Premium subscribers can watch a selection of 2026 Men’s March Madness® games throughout the Tournament, including the 2026 First Round, Second Round, Sweet 16, Elite 8, and Final Four (including the championship) games. To watch March Madness® games on HBO Max: 1. Choose Sports (top of the screen). Here you’ll find live March Madness® games, upcoming games, game replays, and highlights from recent games. 2. Choose the live stream you want to watch. 3. Choose Watch Live to watch the game in progress or Watch from Start (image: restart) to start watching from the beginning.

You can find a list of upcoming March Madness® games by choosing Sports in HBO Max. Replays of March Madness® games are available on HBO Max for a limited time after the live broadcast. Note There are no blackout restrictions for March Madness® games. For more info on watching sports on HBO Max, go to Sports on HBO Max. When more than one March Madness® game is broadcast on HBO Max at the same time, you can choose from pre-set Multiview streams. Things to know about Multiview: 1. Multiview: To watch more action on one screen, choose a pre-set Multiview stream (go to Sports and choose a live sport with Multiview). 2. Supported devices: Multiview is available on computers and TV devices. 3. Change focus: Use the directional buttons on your TV remote (or computer keyboard) to switch between views. 4. Audio: By default, audio plays for the top-left view. When you change the focus, the audio changes to the focused view. 5. Full screen: Switch the focus to the view you want to expand, then press the select button on your TV remote (Enter on your computer keyboard) to enter Full screen. To return to the Multiview stream, go back and restart the Multiview. 6. Captions: Captions appear at the bottom of the screen for the focused view. To turn captions on or off, enter Full screen, choose the Audio and Subtitles icon (image: speech bubble), then choose English - CC or Off. For more info, go to Subtitles, captions, and audio. 7. Alerts: Alerts let you know about key moments in other March Madness® games. Select an alert to switch to that game. To turn alerts on or off, choose the Alerts icon in the upper-right corner of the video player.

Note You can’t change which March Madness® games are in the pre-set Multiviews. ##### How do I watch the games that aren’t on HBO Max? You can watch other March Madness® games using the March Madness® Live app or at NCAA.com.

Martin’s ‘Fire & Blood,’ House of the Dragon tells the story of House Targaryen 200 years before the events of Game of Thrones. Ads on HBO Max

Ads on HBO Max

If you subscribe to the Basic with Ads plan, ads will play before and during shows and movies (about 6 minutes of ads per hour). Ad breaks are marked on the progress bar when you pause playback. ##### Stream without ads To stream shows and movies without ads, change your plan to the Standard or Premium plan. To skip promotional trailers that play before a show or movie, choose the Skip button. Live events have ads regardless of your plan. ##### Ads Q&A Ads play during live events. You may also see ads if you’re subscribed to the Basic with Ads plan. First, check which subscription plan you have: 1. In HBO Max, do one of the following:Phone, tablet, or computer: Choose your profile and then Subscription. 2. TV device: Choose the Settings icon (image: cog (lower left)) and then Subscription.(Only Adult profiles can manage subscriptions.) 3. Find your current plan.

If you have the Basic with Ads plan, you can change your plan to Standard or Premium to watch shows and movies without ads. Live events have ads regardless of your plan. For more info about plans and changing your plan, go to HBO Max plans. From time to time, all subscription plans may contain promotional messaging for content or services that relate to HBO Max content but are available outside of HBO Max (like Mortal Kombat and Game of Thrones companion video games) which may play before shows and movies. To skip these promos choose the Skip button. During shows and movies, you can expect about 6 minutes of ads per hour (Basic with Ads plan). When an ad break starts, the number of ads in the ad break appears on screen. There’s also a timer that counts down to the end of the ad. Ad breaks during kids and family content have bumpers to let you know when an ad break starts and ends. You can pause a show or movie to see the location of ad breaks on the progress bar. Sports: The number of ads during live sports will vary. Live sports have the same commercials that air during the TV broadcast of the event on TBS, TNT, or truTV. Live events included with Sports have ads. Live sports from TBS, TNT, and truTV have the same commercials that air during the TV broadcast of the event. Some sports (e.g., cycling events) may have ads before the sports event starts. No, fast forward and skip aren’t available when an ad is playing. If you’d like to watch shows and movies without ads, change your plan to the Standard or Premium plan. Live sports have ads regardless of your plan. Live sports and events have ads regardless of your plan. When you join a live stream during an ad break, you’ll only watch the remainder of the ad break in progress. You can pause an ad break, but you can’t skip ads. ##### Promotional trailers To skip promotional trailers that play before a show or movie, choose the Skip button. You may see a specific brand more often while watching a series, a long movie, or a similar genre (like comedy). Brands can also sponsor shows and movies. When you’re watching sponsored content, you may see a similar ad at the beginning of each episode. You can give us feedback or report an issue with an ad using our feedback form. When you do this, please provide the following info: - The product or brand - The show or movie you were watching

Getting around the HBO Max app

Getting around the HBO Max app

Want to watch something on HBO Max but aren’t sure how to find it? The HBO Max app includes easy ways to find what you’re looking for, plus personalized recommendations and a place to save your favorites. You can navigate the HBO Max app with screen readers, keyboard shortcuts, and Alexa voice commands. For more info, go to Accessibility on HBO Max. When you first open HBO Max, you’re on the Home screen. This is where you’ll find popular movies and TV series, along with your Continue Watching list, My List, featured collections, and recommendations (Recommended For You). You’ll also find options to browse by Series, Movies, HBO, Sports, and What’s New (if available). To get to the Home screen: - TV device: Choose the Home icon (image: house). - Computer: Open the Browse menu (image: 3 horizontal lines) (upper left), then choose Home. - Phone or tablet: Choose the Home icon (image: house).

Browse by brand: Choose Categories (Browse menu, left edge), find the Brand Spotlight row, then choose a brand you’d like (e.g., HBO, DC, HGTV, Magnolia). Browse by genre: Choose Categories (Browse menu, left edge), find the Browse by Genre row, then choose the genre you’d like (e.g., Action, Comedy, Reality). Browse the Top 10: To browse the most popular new releases, find the Top 10 Series and Top 10 Movies rows. Browse by channels: To browse live TV channels (Standard and Premium plans), find the Channels row. Recommended For You & Because You Watched (recommendations): Recommended For You and Because You Watched are rows on the Home screen with show and movie recommendations based on what you’ve watched in the past. You can start watching something from this row or you can add a show or movie recommendation to My List to watch later. To help you find programming you might like, choose the Bell icon (image: bell) in the upper-right corner (phones and tablets only). Here you’ll find personalized suggestions just for you. If you find something interesting, choose the Menu icon (image: three vertical dots) and then Add to My List or More Info (or you can also choose Remove). Note In-app notifications are only available with Adult profiles. In My Stuff, you can find My List and Continue Watching, and My Purchases (pay-per-view purchases). Choose the My Stuff icon (image: badge) to find what you’ve added to My List and what you can continue watching. Choose your profile icon to manage or switch profiles. ##### Kids profiles To learn about parental controls and the Kids experience, go to All about Kids profiles. Looking for a particular show or movie? If you know what you want to watch, you can use Search. Choose the Search icon (image: magnifying glass) and then type any of the following: - a series name - a movie title - a brand (e.g., “HBO”) - a genre (e.g., “comedy”) - a keyword (e.g., “renovation”) - an actor or actress - a creator, director, or writer - an episode title

As you type, you’ll find the most relevant or popular results. Search results only include series and movies that can be watched by the active profile. ##### Apple TV: Using Siri dictation You can use speech-to-text to search for shows or movies on your Apple TV. 1. Open HBO Max on your Apple TV and choose the Search icon (image: magnifying glass). 2. Press and hold the microphone button on your remote. 3. Speak your search term into the remote.

You can search HBO Max by title, genre, actors, and more. Here you can manage your settings, such as playback preferences and autoplay. For more info, go to HBO Max settings.

Pay Per View on HBO Max

HBO Max subscribers can purchase Pay-Per-View (PPV) events at HBOMax.com/aew or the HBO Max app on select devices. Once purchased, you can watch PPV events on a supported device through the HBO Max app and at HBOMax.com. When you purchase a PPV event on HBO Max, you get exclusive replay access to the event for a limited time. ##### Unsupported devices PPV events can’t be purchased or watched on select devices. Replays: To find available replays of all the PPV events you’ve purchased, choose the My Stuff icon (image: badge) and then go to My Purchases. When you purchase a PPV event on HBO Max, you get exclusive replay access to the event for a limited time. Prices vary by event. To find the cost, choose the event in HBO Max and find the Watch for [Price] text (prices don’t include applicable taxes). You can purchase PPV events at HBOMax.com or through the HBO Max app. ###### What you need - An active HBO Max base plan subscription - A device that supports PPV purchase - An accepted payment method

Do one of the following:Choose your payment method and then enter the security code (CVV) from your card.-or- 4. Choose Edit and enter a new payment method.Note New payment methods are used for a one-time PPV charge and won’t be saved to your account. 5. Choose Confirm & Pay Now.You’ll be charged the PPV event price plus applicable taxes. A receipt will be sent to your HBO Max account email.

When the event is live, go to the event page and choose Watch Live. PPV events are billed separately from your HBO Max base plan subscription. PPV events purchased on the web at HBOMax.com are billed by us. PPV events purchased via the HBO Max app are billed by the on-device app store (e.g., Roku). Watch ‘The Last of Us’

Watch ‘The Last of Us’

Based on the critically acclaimed video game of the same name, ‘The Last of Us’ is back for Season 2. The series takes place 20 years after modern civilization has been destroyed. Joel, a hardened survivor, is hired to smuggle Ellie, a 14-year-old girl, out of an oppressive quarantine zone, resulting in a heartbreaking journey in which they depend on each other for survival. Five years after the events of the first season, Joel and Ellie are drawn into a conflict with each other and a world even more dangerous and unpredictable than the one they left behind. There are 7 episodes in season 2. To learn more, go to The Last of Us. ##### Accessibility ‘The Last of Us’ is available with subtitles, closed captions (English and Spanish), Audio Description (select languages), and ASL interpretation. To watch with ASL, you can search for HBO Max ‘The Last of Us (with ASL)’. You’ll see an ASL badge on the image. For more info, go to Accessibility features on HBO Max. ##### Sign in and stream To stream ‘The Last of Us’, sign into HBO Max today. Need help signing in with your HBO Max account? Go to Sign in to HBO Max. If you don’t have HBO Max, see How do I subscribe to HBO Max? Download HBO Max on a supported device and start streaming today.

What’s playing on HBO Max

What’s playing on HBO Max

HBO Max combines the worlds of HBO, the DC Universe, hit movies, and original series together with fan-favorite genres like true crime, reality, food, and comedy. HBO Max includes: - HBO series and movies, Max Originals, and select series and movies from Warner Bros., DC, Cartoon Network, Harry Potter, Adult Swim, HGTV, the Food Network, TLC, ID, Magnolia Network, Discovery, TCM, and more.

To find series available on HBO Max: - TV device: Choose Series or Movies (left edge). - Phone, tablet, or computer: Open the Browse menu (image: 3 horizontal lines), then choose Series or Movies.

You can also go to HBOMax.com/shows and for movies go to HBOMax.com/movies. ##### Find what’s new Here are some ways to find new titles in HBO Max: - Popular new releases: In HBO Max, find the Top 10 Series and Top 10 Movies rows. - New and coming soon: In HBO Max, find the Just Added and Coming to HBO Max rows. - Check out what’s new and coming soon at HBOMax.com/collections/whats-new. - Choose the Bell icon (image: bell) in HBO Max to get personalized recommendations (phones and tablets only).

Browse by brand

To browse by brand (e.g. DC, HGTV, Magnolia, or Discovery): - TV device: In HBO Max, choose Categories (left edge), find the Brand Spotlight row, then choose a brand. - Phone, tablet, or computer: In HBO Max, open the Browse menu (image: 3 horizontal lines) and choose Categories, find the brand spotlight row, then choose a brand.

You can also search for titles from a brand (e.g., search for ‘Magnolia’). To find HBO titles: - Choose HBO. - Choose Categories (Browse menu, left edge), then find the Brand Spotlight row and choose HBO. - Search for ‘HBO.’

Max Originals are original series and movies produced for HBO Max. To find Max Originals: - Choose Categories (Browse menu, left edge), then find the Brand Spotlight row, and choose Max Originals. - Search for ‘Max Originals.’

To find Discovery favorites: - Choose Categories (Browse menu, left edge), then find the Brand Spotlight row, and choose Discovery. - Search for ‘Discovery.’

  • Choose Sports.
  • Choose Categories (Browse menu, left edge), find the Brand Spotlight row, and choose Sports.
  • Search for ‘Sports.’

To find Warner Bros. shows and movies: - Choose Categories (Browse menu, left edge), then find the Brand Spotlight row, and choose DC or Harry Potter. - Search for ‘DC’ or a show or movie title.

Browse by genre

To browse by genre (e.g. Action, Comedy, Reality): - TV device: In HBO Max, choose Categories (left edge), find the Browse by Genre row, then choose a genre. - Phone, tablet, or computer: In HBO Max, open the Browse menu (image: 3 horizontal lines) and choose Categories, find the Browse by Genre row, then choose a genre.

To browse Kids & Family titles: - Choose Categories (Browse menu, left edge), find the Browse by Genre row, and choose Kids & Family.

You can create a Kids profile that limits what content ratings your child can watch. When a Kids profile is selected, your child can browse by Movies, Series, Characters and brands like Cartoon Network and Sesame Workshop. You can purchase pay-per-view events on HBO Max. You’ll find live and upcoming pay- per-view events: - On the Home screen, choose Sports - By searching ‘Pay-Per-View,’ ‘PPV’, or the title of the event

Replays: To find replays of PPV events you’ve purchased, choose the My Stuff icon (image: badge) and go to My Purchases. For more info, go to Pay Per View on HBO Max. ##### Shows & movies Q&A Most new HBO shows and movies are available on HBO Max when they air on HBO. To find out when new HBO episodes will be available, go to HBO.com and search for a series by name. The following HBO shows are not available when they air on TV. Instead, they’re added to HBO Max as quickly as possible, typically within 24 hours. Below are estimated times when new episodes will be available on HBO Max. Actual times may vary. - Hard Knocks: Training Camp with the Buffalo Bills: Tuesdays, 9pm ET - Last Week Tonight with John Oliver: Mondays, 2am ET - Real Time with Bill Maher: Saturdays, 7am ET

Some movies and shows are available for a limited time only. We aim to provide the most robust offerings and will continue to bring high-quality programs to our streaming platform. If an episode isn’t available, go to Missing episode on HBO Max. To find out how shows and movies are rated, go to Content ratings on HBO Max. To limit what your kids can watch, go to All about Kids profiles. Here’s how to find out which titles are leaving HBO Max: Phone, tablet, or computer: Choose the search icon then find the Last Chance row. TV Device: Choose What’s New (left edge), then find the Last Chance row. You can share what you’re watching using the HBO Max mobile app: 1. On your phone or tablet, select the movie or series you want to share (you can share a series, but not individual episodes). 2. Choose the Share icon and then the app you want to share to (e.g., Messages, Instagram, Facebook, or X).

Note The Share feature is only available with Adult profiles. HBO Max subscribers can purchase Pay-Per-View (PPV) events at HBOMax.com/aew or the HBO Max app on select devices. Find out more at Pay Per View on HBO Max.

Miscellaneous

Extra members on HBO Max

Extra members on HBO Max

Your HBO Max account is for you and the people you live with. With the Extra Member Add-On, WarnerMedia-billed subscribers can share their HBO Max plan with a friend or family member who doesn’t live with them. A base subscription plan is required. ##### Things to Know - The Extra Member Add-On is available to HBO Max subscribers billed through WarnerMedia that are not part of the Disney+, Hulu, HBO Max Bundle. The add-on is not available to subscribers billed through third-party providers, e.g. app stores and internet, mobile, and TV providers. - Account owners pay $7.99/month (plus applicable taxes) for the Extra Member Add-On. - Account owners invite an extra member to share their subscription plan with them. Extra members can be both new or returning subscribers, and will have their own account, password, and profile. - Extra members need to be 18 years or older and be in the same country where the subscription owner signed up for HBO Max.

What are the extra member benefits?

With the Extra Member Add-On, the extra member can: - Stream from anywhere that HBO Max is available (they don’t need to live with the account owner). - Stream on any supported device (1 device at a time). - Have their own account, password, and 1 profile. - Enjoy the same plan features as the account owner (e.g. if the owner has the Premium plan, the extra member can enjoy 4K Ultra HD video quality on select titles).

The account owner (with the Extra Member Add-On) can transfer an existing HBO Max profile to the extra member’s account (this way the extra member can keep that profile’s watch history and My List). ##### Get or remove the Extra Member Add-On With the Extra Member Add-On, you can share your HBO Max plan with a friend or family member who doesn’t live with you. The Extra Member Add-On is only available to HBO Max subscribers billed directly through WarnerMedia (and are not Disney+, Hulu, HBO Max Bundle subscribers). Here’s how to find who bills you for HBO Max: 1. Do one of the following:Web: Go to HBOMax.com/subscription and sign in (if needed). Can’t sign in? 2. Phone, tablet, or computer: In HBO Max, choose your profile and then Subscription. 3. TV device: In HBO Max, choose the Settings icon (image: cog (lower left)) and then Subscription. 4. Find who your base plan is billed through (top of screen).If your HBO Max subscription is billed through WarnerMedia (and not part of a Disney+, Hulu, HBO Max Bundle), you can add the Extra Member Add-On (see steps below).

The Extra Member Add-On is not available to subscribers billed through third-party providers (e.g. app stores, Disney+, Hulu, HBO Max Bundle subscribers, and internet, mobile, and TV providers). Here’s how to get the Extra Member Add-On: 1. Go to HBOMax.com/subscription and sign in (if needed). Can’t sign in? 2. Choose Get Add-Ons.This option is only available if your base subscription is billed through WarnerMedia. 3. Add the Extra Member Add-On to your plan. 4. Confirm the plan change by choosing Confirm & Pay Now.

You’ll be billed each month until you remove the add-on or cancel your base plan. If you purchased your plan and the Extra Member Add-On on different days, they will be billed separately. You can view your payment receipts at HBOMax.com/subscription. For more info, go to How do I get a payment receipt? ###### Invite an Extra Member You must know the email address of the person you want to share HBO Max with. Extra members need to be 18 years or older and be in the same country where the account owner signed up for HBO Max. Follow these steps to invite an extra member (or, invite them later): 1. Choose Invite Extra Member. 2. Enter your extra member’s first name and email address. 3. Choose New Profile or an existing Adult profile from your account. Kids profiles can’t be transferred. 4. Choose Send Invite.We’ll email your extra member and invite them to share your HBO Max subscription. 5. Ask your extra member to check their inbox for an email from HBO Max (subject line: You’ve been invited to HBO Max as an Extra Member).

If your extra member hasn’t accepted your invitation after a few days, we’ll send them a reminder. Your extra member invitation expires after 7 days. If your invitation expires, we’ll email you so that you can resend the invite or invite someone else. Note Extra members need to be in the country where the subscription owner signed up for HBO Max. 1. Go to HBOMax.com/subscription and sign in (if needed). Can’t sign in? 2. Choose Remove Add-On. 3. Follow the on-screen instructions to remove the add-on.

When you remove the Extra Member Add-On, your extra member can continue streaming until the end of your current add-on billing period. Your base plan is not affected. What happens if I cancel my HBO Max subscription? If you cancel your HBO Max subscription, you and your extra member will lose access to HBO Max when your base plan expires. If you have any remaining days on your Extra Member Add-On, you’ll get a prorated refund for the remaining days on your add-on. ##### Invite or remove an extra member Once you have the Extra Member Add-On, you can invite an extra member: 1. Do one of the following:Go to HBOMax.com/account and sign in (if needed). Can’t sign in? 2. HBO Max app: In HBO Max, choose your profile then Account and select Manage Extra Members. 3. Choose Manage Extra Members. 4. Choose Send Invite. 5. Enter your extra member’s first name and email address. 6. Choose New Profile or an existing Adult profile to transfer to the extra member (this way the extra member can keep that profile’s watch history and My List).You can transfer an Adult profile to another account at any time. 7. Choose Send Invite.We’ll email your extra member an invitation to share your HBO Max subscription. 8. Ask your extra member to check their inbox for an email from HBO Max (subject line: You’ve been invited to HBO Max as an Extra Member).

If your extra member hasn’t accepted your invitation after a few days, we’ll send them a reminder. Your extra member invitation expires after 7 days. If your invitation expires, we’ll email you so that you can resend the invite or invite someone else. Note Currently you can purchase 1 Extra Member Add-On, which allows you to share HBO Max with 1 person who doesn’t live with you. 1. Do one of the following:Go to HBOMax.com/account and sign in (if needed). Can’t sign in? 2. HBO Max app: In HBO Max, choose your profile then Account and select Manage Extra Members. 3. Choose Manage Extra Members. 4. Choose Remove Extra Member and then confirm the removal.

When you remove an extra member, they’ll immediately lose access to HBO Max and we’ll email them to let them know that you’re no longer sharing HBO Max with them. Once you remove an extra member, you can invite someone else. Here’s how to find out if your extra member has accepted your invite: 1. Do one of the following:Go to HBOMax.com/account and sign in (if needed). Can’t sign in? 2. HBO Max app: In HBO Max, choose your profile then Account and select Manage Extra Members. 3. Choose Manage Extra Members. 4. Here you’ll find one of the following:Invite accepted: Your extra member’s name and status. 5. Invite not accepted Options to resend or cancel your invite. 6. If your extra member hasn’t accepted your invite, choose Resend Invite. 7. Ask your extra member to check their inbox for an invitation from HBO Max (subject line: You’ve been invited to HBO Max as an Extra Member). 8. When your extra member accepts the invite, we’ll email you.

If your extra member hasn’t accepted your invitation after a few days, we’ll send them a reminder. Your extra member invitation expires after 7 days. Extra member FAQ
  • You can stream from anywhere that HBO Max is available (you don’t need to live with the account owner).
  • Stream on any supported device (1 device at a time).
  • You get your own account, password, and 1 profile.
  • Enjoy the account owner’s plan features (e.g. if the account owner has the Premium plan, then you can enjoy the Premium plan features).

If you didn’t get an email from HBO Max (subject line: You’ve been invited to HBO Max as an Extra Member), do the following: - Search all inbox folders (including Promotions, Junk, and Spam) for an email from HBO Max (subject line: You’ve been invited to HBO Max as an Extra Member). - Ask the account owner what email address they sent the invitation to. If the email address isn’t correct, ask them to cancel the invite and send a new one.

  1. Check your inbox for an email from HBO Max (subject line: You’ve been invited to HBO Max as an Extra Member).
  2. Open this email and choose Accept Invite.
  3. Create an HBO Max account with your own email and password. Make sure to use an email address that isn’t already associated with an HBO Max account.
  4. Choose Start Streaming and you’re ready to watch HBO Max.

Find the error you’re getting below: - Couldn’t Create Account: You need to be in the country where the subscription owner signed up for HBO Max. If you’re traveling, you’ll need to wait and create your account once you’re in the country where the subscription owner signed up for HBO Max. - Something Went Wrong: The invite link may have expired. Ask the account owner to resend the extra member invitation.

You can subscribe to HBO Max using your existing account. Here’s how: 1. Go to HBOMax.com and sign in (if needed). Can’t sign in? 2. Choose your profile and then Subscription. 3. Choose Change Your Plan. 4. Choose a plan and billing period then continue. 5. Add your payment method and start your subscription. 6. Choose Start Streaming and you’re ready to watch HBO Max.

With your own subscription plan, you can stream on more devices at the same time and create additional profiles. We’ll let the person who shared HBO Max with you know that you have your own subscription, so they can invite someone else.
